Theoretically, we prove that EvoDS's hierarchical design reduces tool-selection error, and its optimization objective aligns with an information bottleneck principle, ensuring efficient context use. Empirically, EvoDS outperforms state-of-the-art open-source data science agents by an average of 28.9% across four diverse benchmarks while eliminating out-of-token failures. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/usail-hkust/EvoDS.

</details>


### [75] [PyraMathBench: Evaluating and Improving Mathematical Capability in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03858)
*Zetian Ouyang,Linlin Wang,Gerard de Melo,Liang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PyraMathBench是一个包含32,505道数学题的层次化基准测试，评估LLM的数值推理能力。提出SOLVE模块和IRPO优化方法，通过高效工具调用提升数值-数学协同能力，在Qwen-2.5上获得5.0分提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试很少同时评估LLM的数值处理与数学推理能力，导致数学任务中失败原因难以解释。

Method: 构建PyraMathBench层次化基准（32,505题，涵盖4个认知维度、14个子类别、2种模态）；提出SOLVE模块（智能优化与学习型多功能模块）和IRPO（交互式相对策略优化），通过模糊匹配和低质量调用拒绝等高效工具调用增强数值-数学协同。

Result: LLM在数值计算不足和抽象数值问题处理上表现严重受限；Qwen-2.5经SOLVE和IRPO训练后获得5.0分提升。

Conclusion: 数值推理是LLM数学能力的瓶颈，所提方法能有效提升数值-数学协同能力。

Abstract: Despite the pivotal role of numerical reasoning as the cornerstone of mathematical capabilities in large language models (LLMs) across applications, few benchmarks evaluate LLMs by integrating numerical processing and mathematical reasoning, hindering the interpretability of failures in math tasks. We introduce PyraMathBench, a comprehensive hierarchical benchmark with 32,505 questions derived from 7,404 math word problems, spanning 4 key cognitive aspects, 14 subcategories, and 2 modalities. Experiments reveal that LLMs' performance is severely compromised by inadequate numerical computation and weak handling of abstract numerical questions. To address this, we propose the Smart Optimization & Learning-based VErsatile module (SOLVE) and Interactive Relative Policy Optimization (IRPO), which enhance LLMs' numerical-mathematical synergy via efficient tool calls (fuzzy matching and low-quality call rejection). Comparative experiments show Qwen-2.5 achieves a 5.0 score improvement with SOLVE and IRPO training.

</details>


### [76] [Reasoning Structure of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03883)
*Frédéric Berdoz,Luca A. Lanzendörfer,Fabian Farestam,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将LLM推理过程转化为可验证推理图的方法，并基于逻辑谜题构建了可扩展基准，通过推理拓扑结构定义效率指标，揭示token计数和准确率无法区分的推理行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估指标（如最终答案准确率和token计数）无法捕捉不同推理结构之间的本质差异，相同分数可能掩盖截然不同的推理路径，因此需要一种能度量推理结构的方法。

Method: 1. 构建可扩展的逻辑谜题基准；2. 设计pipeline将非结构化推理轨迹转化为可验证的推理图（包含声明和依赖关系）；3. 基于推理图的拓扑结构定义推理效率指标。

Result: 在开源推理模型上的分析表明，结构度量能够区分token计数和准确率无法区分的推理行为差异。

Conclusion: 所提出的推理图方法和效率指标可作为诊断模型失败模式和比较推理随难度扩展行为的实用工具。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) are often evaluated using metrics such as final-answer accuracy or token count. However, identical scores on these metrics can hide fundamentally different reasoning structures. To address this limitation, we introduce a scalable LRM benchmark of logic puzzles and a pipeline that converts unstructured traces into verifiable reasoning graphs of claims and dependencies. This turns reasoning into a structured, measurable object whose topology can be quantitatively analyzed. Building on this, we define a reasoning efficiency metric that quantifies how concentrated the model's logical flow is. Our analysis on open-source reasoning models shows that structural measurements separate behaviors that token count and accuracy conflate, providing a practical tool for diagnosing failure modes and comparing how reasoning scales with puzzle difficulty.

</details>


### [77] [scTranslation: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Single-Cell Multi-Omics Modality Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03906)
*Jiabei Cheng,Jingbo Zhou,Jun Xia,Changkai Li,Zhen Lei,Chang Yu,Stan Z. Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: scTranslation是一个针对单细胞多组学模态翻译任务的综合基准测试平台，包含多样化的翻译数据集、集成了最先进的模型和全面的评估指标，并系统研究了特征选择、特征质量和少样本设置等影响因素对模型性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有多种单细胞多组学模态翻译计算方法，但缺乏系统的基准评估，包括标准化的数据集、评估指标和影响因素的全面研究。

Method: 构建了scTranslation基准平台，包含多个翻译数据集、集成现有先进模型、提供全面评估指标，并在特征选择、特征质量和少样本等不同场景下评估模型性能。

Result: 通过大规模实验研究，报告了许多有洞察力的发现，揭示了特征选择、特征质量和少样本设置等因素对模型性能的显著影响。

Conclusion: scTranslation填补了单细胞多组学模态翻译领域缺乏系统基准评估的空白，为未来方法开发提供了新方向，并已开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Simultaneous measurement of multiple omics modalities in single cells enables researchers to gain a more comprehensive understanding of cellular states and regulatory mechanisms. However, due to high experimental costs, significant noise, and incomplete modality coverage, a variety of computational methods for modality translation have emerged in recent years. Despite the development of translation models, there is still a lack of systematic benchmark evaluation in terms of datasets, evaluation metrics, and influencing factors. To address this, we present scTranslation, a comprehensive benchmark for single-cell multi-omics modality translation tasks. It includes diverse translation datasets, integrates state-of-the-art models, and provides a comprehensive evaluation metrics. In addition, we assess model performance under different scenarios, such as feature selection, feature quality, and few-shot settings. These factors significantly affect model performance but have rarely been systematically studied before. Leveraging this benchmark, we conduct a large-scale study of current methods, report many insightful findings that open up new possibilities for future development. The benchmark is open-sourced to facilitate future research. The code is anonymously released at https://github.com/Bunnybeibei/scTranslation.

</details>


### [78] [Hedge-Bench: Benchmarking Agents on Hard, Realistic Tasks Pertaining to Financial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03918)
*Eric Cho,Shawn Huang,Alice Lu,Andy Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Hedge-Bench 1.0 是一个包含102个真实对冲基金分析师工作任务的基准测试，基于专家推理轨迹实现确定性评分。前沿模型和智能体在该基准上得分低于16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法捕捉金融分析中开放性问题推理的挑战，且依赖模型评判的评估方法存在噪声和循环论证问题。

Method: 收集102个真实对冲基金分析师的实际工作任务，基于专业分析师的显式推理轨迹构建基准，实现确定性评分而非模型评判。

Result: 前沿模型和智能体在该基准上得分低于16%。

Conclusion: Hedge-Bench 1.0 为评估金融分析中的开放性问题推理提供了更可靠的基准，当前AI系统在此类任务上表现有限。

Abstract: AI agents can increasingly handle the mechanical tasks of financial analysis: retrieving documents, calculating formulas, updating spreadsheets. The harder, more valuable challenge is reasoning through the open-ended questions that define expert Analyst work. Existing benchmarks do not capture this class of problem, and those that attempt to evaluate open-ended reasoning rely on model-judged outputs that introduce noise and circularity. We present Hedge-Bench 1.0: a benchmark of 102 actual, on-the-job tasks grounded in the explicit reasoning traces of professional hedge fund analysts working with relevant information sources. This approach enables deterministic grading against verified expert steps. Frontier models and agents score below 16\% on the benchmark. We publish the dataset and evaluation harness at github.com/Trata-Inc/trata-hedge-bench.

</details>


### [79] [Entropy Is Not Enough: Unlocking Effective Reinforcement Learning for Visual Reasoning via Vision-Anchored Token Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03937)
*Senjie Jin,Peixin Wang,Boyang Liu,Xiaoran Fan,Shuo Li,Zhiheng Xi,Jiazheng Zhang,Yuhao Zhou,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉推理中基于token熵的信用分配机制失效，并提出VEPO框架，通过视觉敏感度与熵的乘法耦合来重新引导梯度信用，显著提升了多模态RL性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在文本RLVR中token熵被广泛用于信用分配，但在视觉推理中该机制是否仍然有效尚不清楚。现有多模态RL方法虽然意识到视觉感知的重要性，但未能有效结合视觉感知和语义推理，要么缺乏系统性的视觉度量，要么忽略了token熵主要驱动语义探索这一事实。

Method: 提出VEPO框架，通过乘法耦合的方式显式整合视觉敏感度与token熵：将梯度信用重新导向同时具备视觉基础和高信息量的token，从而在策略优化中兼顾视觉感知和语义探索。

Result: VEPO在7B规模上以2.28分、3B规模上以3.15分的优势显著超越纯熵基线，消融实验进一步验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 视觉推理中因视觉敏感token具有低熵特性导致熵驱动机制失效，VEPO通过视觉-熵耦合成功解决了这一问题，为多模态RL提供了更有效的信用分配方案。

Abstract: While token-level entropy is commonly recognized as effective for credit assignment in text-only reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), it remains unclear whether this mechanism still holds in visual reasoning. Our controlled study shows that this mechanism collapses in visual reasoning due to the omission of vision-sensitive tokens with naturally low entropy. Although existing multimodal RL methods increasingly acknowledge the importance of visual perception, they struggle to satisfy the inherent demand for interleaving precise perceptual grounding with semantic reasoning, either lacking systematic visual measurements or overlooking that token entropy primarily drives semantic exploration. To address this, we introduce VEPO (Vision-Entropy token-selection for Policy Optimization), an effective RL framework explicitly integrating visual sensitivity with token entropy via a principled multiplicative coupling, where VEPO redirects gradient credit toward tokens which are simultaneously visually grounded and highly informative. Extensive experiments demonstrate VEPO's leading performance, significantly outperforming the entropy-only baseline by 2.28 points at 7B-scale and 3.15 points at 3B-scale. Ablations further substantiate the soundness of our method.

</details>


### [80] [Imaginative Perception Tokens Enhance Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03988)
*Mahtab Bigverdi,Lindsey Li,Weikai Huang,Yiming Liu,Jaemin Cho,Jieyu Zhang,Tuhin Kundu,Chris Dangjoo Kim,Zelun Luo,Linda Shapiro,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出想象感知令牌（IPT），通过引入中间感知表征来增强视觉语言模型在不可见空间配置下的推理能力。在三个新任务（透视、路径追踪、多视角计数）上，IPT监督一致优于文本思维链训练，无需推理时生成图像即可提升空间推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在空间推理中当关键信息不可直接观测时表现不佳，现有方法依赖文本思维链存在模态不匹配问题，需要一种能外部化替代空间配置下感知结果的中间表征形式。

Method: 提出Imaginative Perception Tokens (IPT)，作为中间感知表征层，将VLM在替代空间配置下的感知结果外部化，同时保持与观测输入一致。构建三个任务（PET、PT、MVC）约2万样本数据集，以BAGEL为骨干模型进行IPT监督训练。

Result: IPT监督一致提升空间推理能力，在MVC任务上准确率提升3.4%，在PT任务上达到与强闭源模型竞争的性能。IPT+标签监督获得额外增益，而文本思维链训练可能显著降低性能。

Conclusion: IPT为推理不可观测空间结构提供了原则性的监督信号，能提升泛化能力并产生可解释的中间表征，且避免了语言模态强迫空间计算带来的不匹配问题。

Abstract: Vision language models (VLMs) excel at many tasks but still struggle with spatial reasoning when critical information is not directly observable. Many such problems require imaginative perception: inferring what would be seen from an unseen viewpoint, tracing paths through occluded spaces, or integrating partial observations into a coherent spatial representation. We introduce Imaginative Perception Tokens (IPT), intermediate perceptual representations that externalize what a VLM would perceive under alternative spatial configurations while remaining consistent with the observed input.
  To study this capability, we formulate three tasks, Perspective Taking (PET), Path Tracing (PT), and Multiview Counting (MVC), and construct datasets of approximately 20K examples with ground truth imaginations, answers, and evaluation benchmarks. Using the unified VLM BAGEL as the backbone, IPT supervision consistently improves spatial reasoning and often outperforms textual chain of thought training, even without generating images at inference time. On MVC, IPT improves accuracy by 3.4% and achieves competitive performance with strong closed-source models on PT. We further find that combining IPT and label-only supervision yields additional gains, whereas textual chain of thought can substantially degrade performance, suggesting a modality mismatch when spatial computation is forced through language. Overall, IPT provides a principled supervision signal for reasoning about unobserved spatial structure, improving generalization while producing interpretable intermediate representations.

</details>


### [81] [VESTA: Visual Exploration with Statistical Tool Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00384)
*William Rudman,Abhishek Divekar,Kanishk Jain,Sebastian Joseph,Stella S. R. Offner,Matthew Lease,Kyle Mahowald,Greg Durrett,Junyi Jessy Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VESTA是一个赋予视觉语言模型动态工具创建能力的框架，通过在模型精炼过程中主动探索数据、生成诊断工具（数据变换、可视化、统计检验），在分布拟合和时间序列建模任务上显著优于现有基于迭代批评的智能体系统。作者还引入了DAWN基准测试，涵盖从简单到真实天文学任务的多个难度等级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量模型拟合是科学工作流程的核心步骤，但自动化程度极低。现有基于VLM的智能体系统在复杂建模任务上表现不佳，缺乏主动探索数据的能力，仅依赖迭代批评来修正模型。

Method: 提出VESTA框架，核心创新是（1）动态增长的探索工具包，包含数据变换、假设驱动可视化和统计检验三类工具；（2）VLM在模型精炼前后主动选择或创建诊断工具；（3）生成的工具可积累在上下文中复用。在三个工具配置（无工具、静态专家工具、动态模型生成工具）下进行评估。

Result: 动态工具创建方式在复杂和领域特定任务上显著优于现有智能体流水线；动态生成的工具比现有可视化工具创建系统更复杂，每个函数覆盖更多诊断类别，且更倾向于生成VLM批评者可直接推理的可视化输出。

Conclusion: 主动数据探索和动态工具创建是提升VLM驱动科学建模能力的关键方向，尤其在需要领域知识的复杂建模任务中。

Abstract: Fitting quantitative models to data is a central step in scientific workflows, yet it remains one of the least automated. Recent agent-based systems leverage language and vision-language models (VLMs) to iteratively propose and refine statistical models, but these systems struggle on more challenging modeling tasks. To address these limitations, we introduce VESTA: Visual Exploration with Statistical Tool Agents, a framework that equips VLMs with a dynamically growing exploration toolkit to guide model refinement through data transformations, hypothesis-driven visualizations, and robust statistical tests. Unlike prior systems that rely on iterative critique alone, VESTA actively explores data before and during refinement by selecting or creating diagnostic tools, which accumulate in the model's context and can be reused later. We evaluate VESTA against established baselines in three toolkit configurations: no tools, static expert-written tools, and dynamic model-written tools. To support this evaluation, we introduce DAWN (Dataset for Automated Workflows and Numerical Modeling), a benchmark targeting distribution fitting and time series modeling with varying difficulty tiers, and culminating in real-world astronomy tasks including modeling initial mass functions and gravitational-wave chirp signals. We find that VESTA's dynamic tool creation outperforms prior agentic pipelines, with the largest gains on complex and domain-specific tasks. We further show that dynamically generated tools are substantially more sophisticated than those produced by existing visual tool-creation systems, covering more diagnostic categories per function and strongly preferring visual outputs that the VLM critic can reason over directly.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [82] [Human-in-the-Loop Contextual Bandits for Short-Term Rental Dynamic Pricing: Structural Equivalence of Historical Warm-Up and Approval-Gated Live Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02595)
*Oleg Miroshnichenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HITL-GB框架，通过人类审核约束将历史定价数据转化为在线学习算法的预热数据，将冷启动期从约150轮压缩至30轮，并论证该结构在多个高风险领域具有普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期租赁市场的动态定价面临三大挑战：定价决策财务风险高、算法需要可解释性、市场反馈稀疏（每夜仅一个预订结果）。纯在线bandit学习在稀疏反馈场景下冷启动期过长（数周至数月），不切实际。

Method: 提出Human-in-the-Loop Gated Bandit (HITL-GB)框架：contextual bandit生成价格建议，人类审核者有权接受、修改或拒绝建议。证明在审批约束下，历史定价数据（先验确定性策略收集）结构上等价于在线策略预热数据，可用于初始化bandit后验。形式化定义了审批门控奖励信号，推导了基于正则化岭回归的历史数据预热过程。

Result: 在真实STR生产数据（匿名城市市场，2间房源，2022年4月至2026年4月，1461个夜间定价轮次）上验证：预热程序将Hierarchical Factored Thompson Sampling (HF-TS)代理的有效冷启动从约150轮压缩至约30轮。

Conclusion: 该结构等价性结果是领域无关的：任何需要人类审批的高风险领域（临床药物剂量、信贷审批、内容审核、放射诊断）均满足相同条件并受益于该预热策略。在受监管行业中，强制性人类监督是统计资产而非部署约束。

Abstract: Dynamic pricing in short-term rental (STR) markets presents a distinctive challenge for online learning algorithms: pricing decisions carry significant financial risk, operators require explainability, and market feedback is sparse (one booking outcome per listed night). We introduce the Human-in-the-Loop Gated Bandit (HITL-GB) framework, in which a contextual bandit algorithm generates price recommendations but a human agent retains authority to accept, modify, or reject each recommendation before it is applied. We show that under this approval constraint, historical pricing data -- collected under a prior deterministic policy -- is structurally equivalent to on-policy warm-up data for initialising the bandit's posterior, bypassing the weeks-to-months cold-start period that renders pure online bandit learning impractical in sparse-feedback markets. We formalise the approval-gated reward signal, derive a regularised ridge-regression warm-up procedure from historical episodes, and validate the approach on real STR production data (anonymised urban market, 2 rooms, April 2022 -- April 2026, 1,461 nightly pricing episodes). Our warm-up procedure compresses effective cold-start from ~150 episodes to ~30 episodes when initialising agents from the Hierarchical Factored Thompson Sampling (HF-TS) family. We further argue that the structural equivalence result is domain-agnostic: any high-stakes domain where human approval is legally or operationally required -- including clinical drug dosing, credit origination, content moderation, and radiological diagnosis -- satisfies the same conditions and benefits from the same warm-up strategy. In regulated industries, mandatory human oversight is thus a statistical asset rather than a deployment constraint.

</details>


### [83] [Spectral Asymptotics of Neural Network Loss Landscapes: An Exact Decomposition of the Curvature Exponent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02596)
*Anherutowa Calvo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推导了曲率指数α与梯度奇异值、Hessian特征值之间的谱对齐分解α=2+dlogΦ_k/dlogσ_k，将α在不同层类型中的变化归因于Kronecker因子特征基与梯度奇异方向的对齐几何。该分解引出一个无参数的谱传递恒等式s=αγ，实验证明其在93层、5种架构、3个数据集上中位误差仅~2%。此外，基于ζ函数的参与比界表明曲率集中于每层一个方向，并据此设计了架构自适应预条件子T(σ;α)，在视觉基准上Spectral Newton优化器优于AdamW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为什么不同层类型（卷积、Transformer注意力、MLP上投影）中Hessian特征值随梯度奇异值标度的曲率指数α存在系统性差异（卷积≈2，注意力≈1，MLP上投影<1）。

Method: 提出谱对齐分解（Spectral Alignment Decomposition）：α=2+dlogΦ_k/dlogσ_k，其中Φ_k测量Kronecker因子特征基与梯度奇异方向之间的对齐程度。该分解将α的变化归因于几何对齐问题，并通过LayerNorm、残差连接、softmax头等组件进行解析。进一步推导谱传递恒等式s=αγ，并给出参与比的ζ函数上界。

Result: 谱传递恒等式s=αγ在93层、5种架构、3个数据集上验证，中位误差约2%（无自由参数）。曲率集中于每层一个方向。基于T(σ;α)的Spectral Newton优化器在视觉基准上优于AdamW（α≈2场景）。

Conclusion: 曲率指数的变化可由谱对齐几何解释，谱传递恒等式建立了曲率指数、有效梯度秩衰减指数、Hessian衰减指数之间的代数关系，并可用于设计架构自适应的优化预条件子。

Abstract: The curvature exponent $α$ in $h_k \propto σ_k^α$ -- governing how Hessian eigenvalues scale with gradient singular values -- varies systematically across layer types ($α\approx 2$ for convolutions, $\approx 1$ for transformer attention, $< 1$ for MLP up-projections). Why? We prove the Spectral Alignment Decomposition: $α= 2 + d\logΦ_k / d\logσ_k$, where $Φ_k$ measures alignment between Kronecker factor eigenbases and gradient singular directions. This reduces "why does $α$ vary?" to a geometric question we answer for LayerNorm, residual connections, and softmax heads. The decomposition implies a spectral transfer identity $s = αγ$ linking curvature exponent, effective gradient rank-decay $γ$, and Hessian decay exponent $s$. The identity is algebraic; its empirical content is that $α$ and $γ$, fit on independent data (HVPs vs. SVD), recover $s$ to ~2% median error across 93 layers, five architectures, and three datasets -- with no free parameters. A zeta-function bound on participation ratio shows curvature concentrates onto effectively one direction per layer. As a proof of concept, we derive the architecture-adaptive preconditioner $T(σ;α)$ and show that Spectral Newton -- implementing $T$ in the gradient singular basis -- outperforms AdamW on vision benchmarks where $α\approx 2$.

</details>


### [84] [Making Brain-Computer Interfaces More Secure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02597)
*Md Fahimul Kabir Chowdhury,Gahangir Hossain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级卷积神经网络架构，用于提升EEG-based BCI系统在对抗攻击下的鲁棒性。实验结果表明，该模型在两种EEG数据集上相比EEGNet、DeepConvNet和SleepEEGNet等基线模型，在梯度对抗攻击下具有更好的分类性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG-based BCI研究主要集中在提升分类精度，但对安全性和鲁棒性的关注不足。近年来研究表明EEG-based BCI易受对抗攻击，因此需要评估和增强模型对这类扰动的鲁棒性，以确保可靠部署。

Method: 提出一种轻量级自定义CNN架构，在两种EEG数据集上，与EEGNet、DeepConvNet、SleepEEGNet三种新型CNN模型进行对比，在基于梯度的对抗攻击场景下评估模型的对抗鲁棒性。

Result: 实验结果显示，所提出的轻量级模型在对抗扰动下的分类性能持续优于三种基线模型，表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 轻量级架构在增强EEG-based BCI系统在对抗条件下的可靠性方面具有潜力，为未来构建更安全的BCI系统提供了可行方向。

Abstract: The development of brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) based on electroencephalograms (EEGs) has advanced significantly mainly to machine learning. Although the majority of earlier research has been on increasing classification accuracy, relatively little focus has been placed on security and robustness. According to recent research, EEG-based BCIs are susceptible to adversarial attacks, which can cause misdiagnosis due to minute, well-crafted disturbances. Evaluating model robustness against such perturbations is therefore critical for ensuring reliable deployment. In this study, we propose a lightweight custom Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) architecture to investigate adversarial robustness in EEG-based BCIs. The suggested method is assessed using two EEG datasets and contrasted with three novel CNN models tailored to EEG, namely EEGNet, DeepConvNet, and SleepEEGNet, under gradient-based adversarial attack scenarios. According to experimental findings, the suggested model continuously performs better in classification under adversarial perturbations compared to baseline models, indicating improved robustness. These findings highlight the potential of lightweight architectures for enhancing the reliability of EEG-based BCI systems under adversarial conditions.

</details>


### [85] [Assessing Region-Level EEG Contributions to Cognitive Workload Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02598)
*Jacob Wong,Sohan Singh,Prannaya Gupta,Jin Xing Ang,Kritika Johari,U-Xuan Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于脑电图（EEG）的认知负荷预测区域级评估框架，通过对四个公开数据集的跨任务、跨硬件、跨电极布局的大规模分析，发现额叶和前额中央电极组在混合被试和独立于被试的评估协议下均优于全头皮基线15-20%的排名表现，而后部和枕部区域贡献一致性较差，表明工作负荷相关的EEG信息最稳定地保留在额叶和前额中央区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管EEG被广泛用于工作负荷评估，但区域级EEG贡献在任务、数据集和被试之间的一致性尚不清楚，缺乏统一的评估框架来指导高效且泛化的EEG工作负荷监测系统设计。

Method: 构建区域级评估框架：从解剖学定义的各头皮区域电极提取特征，采用与模型无关的、基于性能的重要性量化方法，在混合被试和独立于被试两种评估协议下进行实验，并使用基于排名的策略聚合结果以确保鲁棒性。

Result: 在所有数据集和独立于被试的评估中，额叶电极组在全头皮基线基础上相对排名提升约15-20%，且使用的电极数量大幅减少；额中央区域表现出最稳定的预测效用，后部和枕部区域在不同实验条件下贡献一致性较差。

Conclusion: 工作负荷相关的EEG信息最稳定地保留在额叶和额中央电极组中，这支持设计高效且泛化的基于EEG的工作负荷监测系统。

Abstract: Accurate and generalizable estimation of cognitive workload from electroencephalography (EEG) is critical for human-centered and safety-critical systems. Although EEG is widely used for workload assessment, the consistency of region-level EEG contributions across tasks, datasets, and subjects remains unclear. This paper presents a region-level evaluation framework for EEG-based workload prediction in which models are trained and evaluated using features extracted exclusively from electrodes belonging to anatomically defined scalp regions. We perform a large-scale analysis across four publicly available EEG workload datasets spanning diverse task demands, recording hardware, and electrode montages. Region importance is quantified using a model-agnostic, performance-based approach under both mixed-subject and subject-independent evaluation protocols, with results aggregated using a rank-based strategy to ensure robustness across experimental configurations. Across all datasets and subject-independent evaluations, frontal electrode groups outperform the full-scalp baseline by approximately 15-20% in relative rank position while using substantially fewer electrodes. Fronto-central regions exhibit the most stable predictive utility, whereas posterior and occipital regions contribute less consistently across experimental conditions. These findings indicate that workload-relevant EEG information is most consistently retained within frontal and fronto-central electrode groups, supporting the design of efficient and generalizable EEG-based workload monitoring systems.

</details>


### [86] [Testing the Test: Score-Direction Instability in Class-Split Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02601)
*Alejandro Ascarate,Leo Lebrat,Rodrigo Santa Cruz,Clinton Fookes,Olivier Salvado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现基于类分割的异常检测评估协议在异常类别与正常分布在表示空间重叠时可能失效，导致分数不可靠甚至反转；提出无需训练的邻域类泄漏诊断方法，能预测分数方向不稳定性；建议将此类基准视为几何依赖的压力测试而非无条件的异常检测能力证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示广泛使用的类分割评估协议在表示空间重叠条件下的病态问题，指出其可能给出误导性结论，并呼吁重新审视该评估范式的有效性。

Method: 分析类分割评估在表示空间重叠时的失败机制；提出一种无需训练的简单诊断指标——邻域类泄漏（Neighborhood Class Leakage）；在Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10和Imagenette数据集上，于像素空间和VAE潜空间中进行实验验证。

Result: 邻域类泄漏指标能够有效预测异常检测分数方向的不稳定性；在表示空间重叠时，异常分数可能趋近随机甚至发生反转；最优分数方向依赖于未知的异常类别。

Conclusion: 类分割异常检测基准应被视为依赖几何结构的压力测试，而非无条件的异常检测能力证据。

Abstract: Within-dataset class-split evaluation is widely used as a proxy for fully unconditional out-of-distribution anomaly detection. We show that this protocol can become ill-posed when the held-out anomaly class overlaps the normal mixture in representation space. In this regime, anomaly scores may collapse toward chance or even invert, and the preferred score direction can depend on the unknown anomaly class. We introduce a simple training-free diagnostic, neighborhood class leakage, and show that it predicts score-direction instability across Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, and Imagenette, in both pixel and VAE latent spaces. Our results suggest that class-split AD benchmarks should be treated as geometry-dependent stress tests rather than unconditional evidence of anomaly-detection ability.

</details>


### [87] [Graph Mamba Survival Analysis Based on Topology-Aware ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02602)
*Yuanfang Chen,Peiqiang Yan,Yuntao Shou,Qian Zhao,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TopoMamSurv，一种基于拓扑感知排序的图Mamba生存分析框架。通过拓扑感知排序策略（TAO）解决Mamba对输入数据顺序敏感的问题，并设计双向Mamba模块结合GCN实现"局部聚合-全局捕获"的分层特征学习。在五个TCGA数据集上验证了性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSIs生存分析面临三大挑战：Transformer的O(N²)计算瓶颈、图Mamba中传统节点排序方法未考虑图拓扑连通性导致序列建模性能受限、单向Mamba结构无法利用图像双向空间结构。

Method: 提出TopoMamSurv框架，包括：(1) 拓扑感知排序策略（TAO），通过考虑图拓扑连通性进行节点排序以适应Mamba的顺序敏感性；(2) 双向Mamba模块，实现双向空间上下文建模；(3) 集成GCN形成"局部聚合-全局捕获"的分层特征学习架构。

Result: 可视化实验证实TAO策略提取的节点具有更高相似度。在五个TCGA数据集上验证了框架的全面性能优势。

Conclusion: 该框架通过TAO、双向语义建模和分层特征融合的系统性设计，有效解决了WSIs分析中长程依赖建模、计算效率与空间结构利用之间的矛盾。

Abstract: In computational pathology, Whole Slide Images (WSIs) survival analysis is crucial for patient prognosis assessment, but it faces multiple technical challenges. Although the Transformer captures long-range dependencies through its self-attention mechanism, its $O(N^2)$ time complexity causes a severe computational bottleneck in large-scale WSIs graph structures. The Mamba model breaks through the Transformer's computational bottleneck with linear complexity. But, owing to Mamba's high sensitivity to the order of input data, traditional node sorting methods in Graph Mamba, such as those based on node degree or subgraph size, fail to adequately account for the topological connectivity of graph data. This inadequacy consequently restricts the performance of Mamba's sequential modeling. Moreover, its unidirectional architecture cannot leverage the bidirectional spatial structure of images. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a novel Graph Mamba survival analysis framework based on topology-aware ordering (TopoMamSurv) to adapt to the sequential sensitivity of Mamba. Our visualization experiments further confirmed that the nodes extracted through the topology-aware ordering (TAO) strategy indeed exhibit higher similarity. Furthermore, we designed a bidirectional Mamba module and integrated a Graph Convolutional Network (GCN) to achieve bidirectional spatial context modeling of images, forming a hierarchical feature learning architecture for "local aggregation - global capture." This framework effectively reconciles the contradiction between long-range dependency modeling, computational efficiency, and spatial structure utilization in WSIs analysis through its systematic design of TAO, bidirectional semantic modeling, and hierarchical feature fusion. This framework has been validated for its comprehensive performance advantage on five TCGA datasets.

</details>


### [88] [Auditable Climate Risk Intelligence from Fragmented ESG Data: Deterministic Orchestration and Imbalance-Aware Learning for Scope 1-3 Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02604)
*Karan Sehgal,Khawar Naveed Bhatti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个确定性气候风险智能框架，整合单一事实源编排、时间异常检测、不平衡感知集成学习和可解释性治理，用于可审计的ESG验证，并在合成基准数据集上评估，结果优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ESG和气候风险数据在Scope 1/2/3报告环境中碎片化严重，传统验证流程缺乏来源可感知的审计能力、隐藏漂移检测和面向可复现性的治理机制。

Method: 提出确定性气候风险智能框架，包含：时序漂移分析、SMOTE稀有事件优化、集成学习、来源感知编排、TreeSHAP可解释性治理；并构建了基于GHG Protocol/PCAF/ISSB标准的合成ESG验证基准数据集。

Result: 基于分类指标(recall, F1, ROC AUC)、校准指标(ECE, Brier score)和治理导向审计轨迹完整性指标，在五折交叉验证中进行评估，并配以配对显著性检验。

Conclusion: 该框架将ESG报告重塑为确定性的气候风险治理基础设施，支持可复现性、可解释性和可操作审计性。

Abstract: ESG and climate risk data remain fragmented across heterogeneous Scope 1, Scope 2, and Scope 3 reporting environments, while conventional validation pipelines lack provenance aware auditability, hidden drift detection, and reproducibility oriented governance. This paper proposes a deterministic climate risk intelligence framework integrating single source of truth orchestration, temporal anomaly detection, imbalance aware ensemble learning, and explainability oriented governance for auditable ESG validation. To support open reproducibility, we construct and release a synthetic ESG validation benchmark calibrated against publicly reported characteristics of the GHG Protocol, PCAF, and ISSB standards. The methodology incorporates temporal drift analysis, SMOTE based rare event optimization, ensemble learning, provenance aware orchestration, and TreeSHAP based interpretability for governance inspection and audit reconstruction. We evaluate the framework against statistical classifiers, anomaly detection methods, temporal forecasting baselines, and a threshold based system using classification metrics (recall, F1, ROC AUC), calibration metrics (ECE, Brier score), and a governance oriented audit trace completeness metric measuring the fraction of flagged anomalies for which a deterministic source to escalation provenance chain can be reconstructed. Results are reported as mean and standard deviation across stratified five fold cross validation with paired significance testing. The framework reframes ESG reporting toward deterministic climate risk governance infrastructure supporting reproducibility, explainability, and operational auditability.

</details>


### [89] [Cross-Modal Contrastive Learning of ECG and Angiography Representations for Severe Stenosis Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02605)
*Nikola Cenikj,Özgün Turgut,Alexander Müller,Alexander Steger,Jan Kehrer,Marcus Brugger,Daniel Rueckert,Philip Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StenCE是一个基于心电图(ECG)的冠状动脉狭窄预训练框架，通过从ECG中提取特征实现狭窄风险分层，在多种狭窄严重度阈值和心电图疾病分类任务上优于先前方法，首次在严重狭窄分类上取得高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冠状动脉狭窄诊断依赖有创的冠状动脉造影，但该方法仅用于高风险患者，导致无症状患者可能漏诊。心电图虽快速、廉价、无创且常规采集，但缺乏可靠的狭窄特异性信号，无法用于狭窄风险分层。因此，需要一种能从ECG中提取狭窄相关特征的方法。

Method: 提出StenCE预训练框架，利用ECG数据直接提取特征进行患者风险分层。通过预训练策略使模型学习ECG中与狭窄相关的信号表示。

Result: 在不同狭窄严重度阈值和额外ECG疾病分类任务上，StenCE在不同ECG编码器上均表现出一致的性能提升，优于先前工作，并首次在严重狭窄分类上达到高性能。

Conclusion: StenCE成功检测到ECG中用于狭窄诊断的信号，证明ECG可用于冠状动脉狭窄风险分层，支持早期诊断，尤其对无症状患者具有临床价值。

Abstract: Coronary artery stenosis is a common cardiovascular disease, with severe, untreated cases posing significant risks of heart attack. Although coronary (X-ray) angiograms remain the standard for stenosis diagnosis, they are invasive, time- and resource-intensive, and therefore only performed on patients with a high probability of disease based on symptoms and prior clinical tests. However, a subset of patients, especially those without symptoms, may remain undiagnosed. Detecting indications of stenosis from ECGs, which are fast, cheap, non-invasive, and thus routinely acquired even in asymptomatic patients, would support early diagnosis. However, as no reliable stenosis-specific signal has been identified in ECGs, they can not currently be used for stenosis risk stratification. To address this, we introduce StenCE, a pretraining framework, allowing stratification of patients based on features derived directly from ECGs. Evaluations across varying stenosis severity thresholds and additional ECG disease classification tasks demonstrate consistent performance improvements across different ECG encoders, outperforming previous work. The obtained models successfully detect signals for stenosis diagnosis in ECGs and are the first to achieve high performance in severe stenosis classification. The source code is available at https://github.com/NikolaCenic/ecg-stenosis-cls.

</details>


### [90] [ReLoRA: Knowledge-Reusing Adaptation for Fast Rollout of Evolving LLM Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02606)
*Yang Xu,Zihuai Xu,Hongli Xu,Yunming Liao,Zhiwei Yao,Xitong Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReLoRA框架，通过自适应初始化（贝叶斯优化融合旧适配器与基模型演化信息）和分阶段调度正则化微调，实现大语言模型基座更新后LoRA适配器的高效复用，相比从头训练加速最高8.9倍，准确率提升最高4.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为持续演进的在线服务，基座模型频繁更新会导致先前部署的任务专用LoRA适配器失效。从头重训练所有适配器计算成本极高且延迟服务部署，而直接复用则因适配器与更新后基座不兼容导致服务质量下降。

Method: ReLoRA包含两步优化：1) 自适应LoRA初始化，利用贝叶斯优化融合旧适配器参数与基模型演化信息，构建兼容性感知的初始化起点；2) 分阶段正则化微调，先通过强正则化快速将适配器引导至高质量区域，再放松正则化进行任务特定精调，实现低开销的服务质量快速恢复。

Result: 实验表明ReLoRA相比基线方法，服务就绪时间（time-to-readiness）最高减少8.9倍，准确率最高提升4.6%。

Conclusion: ReLoRA通过知识复用的重适应框架，有效解决了LLM持续演进场景下LoRA适配器的兼容性问题，兼顾了计算效率与任务性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as continuously evolving services, where frequent base-model updates may invalidate previously deployed task-specific Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) adapters. For service providers managing numerous downstream model services, retraining each LoRA adapter from scratch for every updated base model is computationally prohibitive and delays service rollout. Meanwhile, the simpler alternative, i.e., naively applying the original LoRA adapter to the updated base model, often leads to degraded service quality due to adapter-backbone incompatibility. To address this problem, we propose ReLoRA, a knowledge-reusing re-adaptation framework that efficiently restores service-ready LoRA adapters for evolving LLM services while preserving or improving task performance. Specifically, ReLoRA comprises two key optimization steps: 1) Adaptive LoRA initialization leverages Bayesian optimization to construct a compatibility-aware starting point by fusing information from both the previously deployed task adapter and the base model's evolution; 2) Fine-tuning with scheduled regularization first rapidly steers the adapter to a high-quality region via strong regularization, followed by relaxed regularization for task-specific refinement. This design enables rapid service-quality recovery with reduced re-adaptation overhead. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReLoRA reduces time-to-readiness by up to 8.9$\times$ and improves accuracy by up to 4.6\% compared to baselines.

</details>


### [91] [Geometry-Aware Tabular Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02607)
*David Turtora Zagardo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何感知表格扩散模型(GATD)，通过在扩散去噪器中引入列值差异的成对角度和长度作为输入与辅助目标，以显式监督列间关系。MLP版本平均减少3.5倍参数，在10个数据集上取得8/10 Shape、7/10 Trend和9/10下游效用的最优结果，Shape和Trend误差分别降低27%和20%。消融实验表明增益源于监督信号而非额外输入或容量。显式关系监督是一种可迁移的表格扩散归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格扩散模型依赖隐式机制捕捉列间关系，缺乏对表格结构化关系的显式建模，导致合成质量受限。

Method: GATD通过在扩散去噪器中计算列值差异的成对角度和长度，将其作为额外输入和辅助训练目标，以显式监督列间几何关系。该方法可适配MLP、GNN和Transformer等多种去噪器架构。

Result: MLP版本以平均3.5倍更少参数在10个数据集中赢得8/10 Shape、7/10 Trend和9/10下游效用，Shape/Trend误差降低27%/20%。几何监督可迁移至GNN和Transformer，在27/30和25/30的架构-数据集组合上提升Shape和Trend。

Conclusion: 显式关系监督是一种有效的、可迁移的表格扩散归纳偏置，其增益主要来源于监督信号本身而非额外输入或模型容量增加。

Abstract: Tabular synthesis is critical for privacy-preserving sharing and augmentation, yet diffusion models rely on implicit mechanisms to capture inter-column relationships. We introduce Geometry-Aware Tabular Diffusion (GATD), which augments tabular diffusion denoisers with pairwise angles and lengths computed from column value differences and used as inputs and auxiliary targets. Our MLP instantiation achieves state-of-the-art benchmark performance while using 3.5x fewer parameters on average (up to 25x for classification tasks): on ten datasets, it wins 8/10 Shape, 7/10 Trend, and 9/10 downstream utility (F1/RMSE), reducing Shape and Trend error by 27% and 20%. Default loss weights transfer to GNN and Transformer denoisers, improving Shape on 27/30 and Trend on 25/30 architecture-dataset cells. A matched ablation shows supervision (not extra inputs or capacity) drives the gain. This shows explicit relational supervision is a portable inductive bias for tabular diffusion.

</details>


### [92] [Pruning Deep Neural Networks via the Marchenko--Pastur Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02608)
*Leonid Berlyand,Theo Bourdais,Houman Owhad,Yitzchak Shmalo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Marchenko-Pastur随机矩阵理论的深度神经网络剪枝方法，在极小的后剪枝微调预算下实现精度保持。理论提供了确定性数据路径保证，实验表明在ImageNet-1k上，ViT-B/16在$2{:}4$+ToMe稀疏化下达到$83.41\%$ top-1精度（下降1.70 pp），实现$59.81\%$稀疏执行MAC缩减和$1.388\times$ A40加速；结构化稀疏下ViT-L/16 $8{:}16$达到$85.33\%$（下降0.51 pp），ConvNeXtV2-Base $12{:}16$达到$86.35\%$（下降0.37 pp）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度神经网络剪枝方法通常需要冗长的后剪枝重新优化流程，本文旨在探索极短校准和微调预算下的精度保持剪枝方法，以降低实际部署成本。

Method: 采用Marchenko-Pastur随机矩阵方法进行神经网络剪枝，推导出确定性数据路径证书：当移除分量$R$的传播logit效应$L_s \| R ψ_1(s) \|_\infty$很小时，剪枝会降低弹性网络目标并保留稠密边界超过两倍扰动的样本。提出了零预算完美剪枝、剪枝-恢复扩展（固定稀疏执行模式下的权重恢复）和加性$L_2$正则化模型。在iid高斯充分条件下，拟合的MP边缘$σ_+$提供高概率的逐层预算信号。

Result: ImageNet-1k上，ViT-B/16 $2{:}4$+ToMe达到$83.41\%$ top-1（下降1.70 pp），$59.81\%$ MAC缩减，$1.388\times$ A40加速；A100无ToMe端点达$2.705\times$。ViT-B/16 $6{:}12$达$83.74\%$，ViT-L/16 $8{:}16$达$85.33\%$（下降0.51 pp），ConvNeXtV2-Base $12{:}16$达$86.35\%$（下降0.37 pp）。ResNet50 $8{:}16$达$75.87\%$（下降0.26 pp），ResNet152d CAST-conv+permutation达$81.33\%$（下降1.53 pp），${\sim}50\%$ MAC缩减，$1.62\times$ A40加速。

Conclusion: 基于MP随机矩阵理论的剪枝方法能在极短微调预算下有效保持模型精度，理论证明与实验验证一致，为实际部署中的高效剪枝提供了理论指导和实用方案。

Abstract: We study a Marchenko--Pastur (MP) random-matrix approach to pruning deep neural networks with very small post-pruning fine-tuning budgets. The main practical contribution is accuracy retention under short calibration and fine-tuning schedules, rather than a long post-pruning reoptimization pipeline. The theory gives deterministic data-path certificates: if the removed component $R$ has small propagated logit effect $L_s \| R ψ_1(s) \|_\infty$, pruning decreases an elastic-net objective and preserves samples whose dense margin exceeds twice the perturbation. The zero-budget case gives perfect pruning; a prune--restore extension models weight restoration inside a fixed sparse-execution pattern; and an additive $L_2$-regularized model shows admissible random-like components vanish at the training limit, with persistent spikes stabilizing as the MP bulk collapses. Under iid-Gaussian sufficient conditions, the fitted MP edge $σ_+$ gives a high-probability layerwise budget signal.
  On ImageNet-1k, after only three distillation epochs, ViT-B/16 $2{:}4{+}$ToMe reaches $83.41\%$ top-1 ($-1.70$ pp from dense) at $59.81\%$ sparse-execution MAC reduction, with $1.388\times$ best-observed A40 native-$2{:}4$ backend speedup for the same checkpoint and ToMe graph; a separate no-ToMe A100 endpoint gives $2.705\times$. At structured sparsity, ViT-B/16 $6{:}12$ reaches $83.74\%$, ViT-L/16 $8{:}16$ dense+permutation reaches $85.33\%$ ($-0.51$ pp), and ConvNeXtV2-Base $12{:}16$ reaches $86.35\%$ ($-0.37$ pp). For CNNs, ResNet50 $8{:}16$ dense+permutation reaches $75.87\%$ ($-0.26$ pp), and ResNet152d CAST-conv+permutation reaches $81.33\%$ ($-1.53$ pp) at ${\sim}50\%$ MAC accounting with a $1.62\times$ A40 im2col$+2{:}4$ sparse-GEMM audit.

</details>


### [93] [Building Better Activation Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02609)
*Jan Bauer,Celeste De Schamphelaere,Adam Karvonen,Niclas Luick,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文改进了激活预言机的训练流程（包括策略内 rollout、对话数据集改进、多层输入、注入公式优化），并开源了首个综合评估套件 AObench，虽能力提升有限但显著改善了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活预言机存在幻觉、模糊性和文本反转混淆等问题，且缺乏标准化评估手段，阻碍了其在可解释性研究中的应用。

Method: 提出四项训练改进：(1) 策略内 rollout 训练；(2) 改进对话数据集；(3) 馈入更多网络层信息；(4) 优化注入公式。同时构建了 AObench 评估套件。

Result: 能力提升（如准确性）边际效应有限，但用户体验和稳定性的改进较为显著；AObench 提供了标准化的评估基准。

Conclusion: 该工作为激活预言机及端到端可扩展可解释性范式奠定了评估基础，促进了该方向的发展。

Abstract: Activation Oracles (AOs) are promising methods for interpreting residual stream activations. However, current AOs face important issues, such as hallucinations and vagueness. Additionally, text-inversion confounds make them hard to evaluate. To this end, we improve the Activation Oracle (AO) training regime in four ways: training on on-policy rollouts, improving the conversational dataset, feeding more layers and an improvement to the injection formula. The capability improvements are marginal, but quality of life improvements are quite substantial. In addition, we open source the first comprehensive evaluation suite for AO quality, which we call AObench. Overall, we hope that our work sets a foundation that helps improve AOs and other models in the paradigm of scalable, end-to-end interpretability.

</details>


### [94] [GLENS: Global Search via Learning from Solver Iterates with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00366)
*Anjian Li,Bartolomeo Stellato,Ryne Beeson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GLENS是一种数据高效的全局搜索方法，利用求解器中间迭代步作为免费数据增强，通过扩散模型学习局部邻域结构并预测高质量、多样化的初始猜测，以加速多模态非凸连续优化问题的求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法仅利用最终收敛最优解来预测初始猜测，丢弃了求解过程中邻域信息，限制了训练数据量，难以兼顾初始猜测的高质量和多样性。

Method: GLENS包含两个组件：1) 邻域结构模型，使用扩散模型学习条件于问题参数的最优解局部几何结构；2) 求解器行为模型，学习精炼方向以在扩散采样过程中引导样本趋近附近最优解。

Result: 在改进的非凸基准问题和双机器人避障导航问题上，GLENS生成的高质量初始猜测保持了多模态分布，在不同问题设置和求解器下均实现了更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: GLENS通过利用求解器中间迭代步作为数据增强，有效提升了初始猜测的质量和多样性，为多模态非凸优化中的全局搜索提供了一种数据高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We consider the problem of generating a large collection of initial guesses for local minima of multimodal non-convex continuous optimization problems. The goal is for these initial guesses to be high-quality (i.e., a numerical solver converges quickly) and diverse (i.e., represent many different local minima). Identifying multiple locally optimal solutions enables flexible downstream decision-making, but typically requires expensive global search. Existing data-driven methods predict initial guesses using only the final converged optima from offline solver runs, which discards information about the local neighborhoods of solutions and limits the available training data. We propose GLENS (Global Search via Learning from Solver Iterates), a data-efficient global search method that leverages intermediate solver iterates as free data augmentation. GLENS consists of two components: a neighborhood structure model that uses diffusion models to learn the local geometry around optima conditioned on problem parameters, and a solver behavior model that learns refinement directions to further guide samples towards nearby optima during diffusion sampling. Experiments on modified non-convex benchmark problems and a two-robot obstacle-avoidance navigation problem show that GLENS generates high-quality initial guesses while preserving the multimodal distribution of diverse local optima. The resulting initial guesses lead to faster solver convergence across different problem settings and solvers. We also analyze how key hyperparameter choices affect the performance.

</details>


### [95] [Hallucination Is Linearly Decodable from Mid-Layer Hidden States in Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02628)
*Aizierjiang Aiersilan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究开源LLM的隐藏状态中是否存在线性可分的真实性信号。在3个7B-8B指令微调模型上，使用4个幻觉基准测试，发现中层隐藏状态的线性探针可达0.904-1.000 AUROC，而基于采样的检测方法在同一协议下不超过0.541 AUROC。MLP探针相比线性探针提升不超过0.01 AUROC，表明信号近似线性。峰值探针层集中在特定区间。第一块注意力熵在知识驱动场景下提供0.866-0.941 AUROC的补充信号。采样方法表现差源于评估协议的结构性不匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究开源LLM的隐藏状态中是否存在线性可分的真实性信号，并定位该信号在网络中的最强深度位置，以理解LLM内部对真实性的编码机制。

Method: 对3个7B-8B指令微调开源模型（Llama-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B, Qwen2.5-7B）进行4-bit NF4量化，在4个幻觉基准（TruthfulQA, HaluEval-QA, FEVER, 合成集）上提取逐层隐藏状态，比较线性探针、MLP探针、INSIDE EigenScore、自一致性、注意力熵共5种检测方法。

Result: 单层线性探针在中间层达到0.904-1.000 AUROC；采样方法不超过0.541 AUROC；MLP相比线性探针提升不超过0.01 AUROC；峰值层集中在特定区间（Llama/Mistral: 13-18/32层, Qwen: 19-25/28层）；第一块注意力熵在HaluEval-QA上达0.866-0.941 AUROC。

Conclusion: 开源LLM的隐藏状态中编码了线性可分的真实性信号，峰值位于中后层。采样方法表现差源于配对标签评估与这些方法信息访问方式的结构性不匹配，而非方法本身局限。注意力熵可作为零成本补充信号。

Abstract: We investigate whether open-source LLMs encode a linearly separable truthfulness signal in their hidden states, and at which network depth this signal is strongest. Across three $7$B--$8$B instruction-tuned models (Llama-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B, Qwen2.5-7B) loaded in $4$-bit NF4 quantization, we extract per-layer hidden states on four hallucination benchmarks (TruthfulQA, HaluEval-QA, FEVER, and a controlled synthetic set) and compare four detection approaches: linear and MLP probes, INSIDE EigenScore, self-consistency, and attention entropy. A linear probe on a single mid-network layer achieves $0.904$--$1.000$ AUROC on held-out splits, while sampling-based detectors do not exceed $0.541$ AUROC under the same protocol. The truthfulness signal is approximately linear: MLP probes rarely surpass linear probes by more than $0.01$ AUROC. Peak probing layers fall in a consistent band across model families on natural-language benchmarks -- blocks~$13$--$18$ of~$32$ for Llama and Mistral, and blocks~$19$--$25$ of~$28$ for Qwen. First-block attention entropy provides a complementary signal in knowledge-grounded settings ($0.866$--$0.941$ AUROC on HaluEval-QA) at no additional inference cost. The low discriminability of sampling methods under this protocol reflects a structural mismatch between paired-label evaluation and the information these methods access, rather than an inherent limitation of those methods. Code and data are released for full reproducibility on a single $8$\,GB GPU.

</details>


### [96] [Mitigating Spurious Correlations with Memorization-Guided Dataset De-Biasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02830)
*Arda Fazla,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段样本评分函数，通过解耦核心特征与虚假特征的学习动态来分别评估其难度，并基于该指标选择信息性样本进行训练，在仅使用10%原始数据的情况下，超越了现有去偏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实数据集中存在虚假相关性，导致模型依赖虚假特征而误分类少数样本。现有子集选择方法需要组标签且样本评分函数受虚假特征干扰，无法准确捕捉核心因果特征的重要性。

Method: 提出两阶段样本评分函数：首先解耦核心特征与虚假特征的学习动态，然后分别评估两类特征的难度。基于该指标设计算法，优先选择包含/不包含虚假相关性的信息性样本。

Result: 标准ERM模型在所选样本上训练，仅用10%原始数据即达到超越SOTA去偏技术的性能。

Conclusion: 通过解耦特征学习动态并分别评估难度，可以有效选择高质量子集来缓解虚假相关性，无需组标签且数据效率极高。

Abstract: Real-world datasets often contain spurious correlations that are not causally related to the target label. When such correlations dominate the majority of training samples, models tend to rely on them, leading to misclassification of minority samples that do not exhibit the same spurious patterns. While a potential approach is to select subsets of data to better represent the minority samples, this may require access to group labels, which are typically unknown. Furthermore, as we demonstrate, widely used sample scoring functions in the invariant subset or coreset selection literature largely depend on spurious features and therefore fail to accurately capture the importance or difficulty of core, causally relevant features. Accordingly, we propose to mitigate spurious correlations by developing a two-stage sample scoring function that disentangles the learning dynamics of core and spurious features and evaluates their difficulty separately. Based on our proposed metric, we introduce a new algorithm to find and prioritize informative samples both with and without spurious correlations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that a standard ERM model trained on our selected samples achieves superior performance compared to state-of-the-art debiasing techniques, while requiring as little as 10\% of the original training data.

</details>


### [97] [Regime-Arrival Uncertainty in Generalization Bounds under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02657)
*Prince Poudel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个量化机制转换环境下分布偏移导致的额外风险的框架，通过精确分解机制不匹配与机制敏感性，在合成数据和25年全球股票指数上验证了最小最大下界，并指出现有训练-only估计器无法捕捉时间到达特征，因此该框架不是预测机器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准泛化界假设训练和部署分布相同或静态，未考虑平静与危机状态比例不同的机制转换环境，需要量化机制组成不匹配带来的额外风险。

Method: 提出一个泛化机制感知模型的框架，通过精确分解分离机制不匹配与机制敏感性；将界扩展到β-混合数据，使用针对谱间隙校正的有效样本量；推导合成数据和全球股票指数的极小极大下界。

Result: 提出的惩罚项是事后实现的泛化差距，而训练-only估计器未显示显著相关性：危机特征几何可检测，但时间到达不可检测。

Conclusion: 该框架不是预测机器，未来机制组成的预测在机制变化的罕见情况下仍是一个开放问题。

Abstract: The standard generalization bounds assume that the training and deployment distributions are the same, or are static, and don't consider regime switching environments where the ratio of calm vs crisis states is different. This paper proposes a framework that generalizes regime-aware models by quantifying the extra risk due to regime composition mismatch, when distribution shifts are Markov-switching. We obtain an exact decomposition, separating regime mismatch from regime sensitivity; we extend the bound to beta-mixing data using the effective sample size corrected for the spectral gap; and we show a minimax lower bound for synthetic data and on 25 years of global equity indices. The proposed penalty is an ex post realized generalization gap, whereas the training-only estimator does not show significant correlation: the feature geometry of crises can be detected, but not the temporal arrival. Thus, the framework is not a forecast machine. Forecasting the composition of the future regime is an open question in the rare cases of regime change.

</details>


### [98] [A Nonmonotone Gradient-Based Algorithm for Symmetric Nonnegative Matrix Factorization and Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02887)
*Ryan Swart,Johannes Brust*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SNMPBB，首个将非单调投影Barzilai-Borwein方法应用于对称非负矩阵分解的算法，并扩展至图聚类（Graph-SNMPBB）和大规模低秩近似（LAI-SNMPBB）。实验表明，SNMPBB在合成数据上比SymANLS快6倍，Graph-SNMPBB在图聚类基准上匹配或超越SymANLS精度，LAI-SNMPBB在34个SuiteSparse矩阵上优于LAI-SymPGNCG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称NMF的投影梯度方法收敛缓慢，现有梯度算法效果被低估，需要更高效的优化方法。

Method: 引入非单调投影Barzilai-Borwein方法（SNMPBB），扩展至图拉普拉斯正则化版本（Graph-SNMPBB）和低秩近似版本（LAI-SNMPBB），并证明全局收敛到一阶驻点，且BB曲率信息在随机近似下得以保留。

Result: 合成数据上SNMPBB比SymANLS快6倍，高秩优势更明显；Graph-SNMPBB在6个真实图聚类基准上匹配或超越SymANLS精度；LAI-SNMPBB在34个SuiteSparse矩阵上在运行时间和残差质量上均优于LAI-SymPGNCG。

Conclusion: 梯度算法在对称NMF中远比先前认知更有效，所提方法在速度和精度上均具有显著优势。

Abstract: Symmetric nonnegative matrix factorization (Symmetric NMF) approximates a matrix as $WW^T$ with nonnegative rectangular factor $W$. It has broad applications in graph clustering and machine learning. In contrast to the NMF, projected gradient methods for the symmetric problem had been associated with slow convergence. To address this, we introduce SNMPBB, the first adaptation of nonmonotone projected Barzilai-Borwein methods to Symmetric NMF, demonstrating that gradient algorithms are significantly more effective than previously understood. We further extend SNMPBB to graph clustering using the graph Laplacian regularization (Graph-SNMPBB) and to large problems with low-rank approximations (LAI-SNMPBB). For all variants we prove global convergence to first-order stationary points and also that Barzilai-Borwein curvature information is preserved with randomized approximations. On synthetic data, SNMPBB achieves 6 times speedup over the alternative SymANLS for similar residuals, with advantages growing at higher ranks. Across six real-world clustering benchmarks, Graph-SNMPBB matches or exceeds SymANLS accuracy. Lastly, LAI-SNMPBB outperforms state-of-the-art LAI-SymPGNCG on 34 SuiteSparse matrices in both runtime and residual quality.

</details>


### [99] [CL-DMDF:Dynamic Multimodal Data Fusion Model Based on Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02659)
*Dong Li,Lingling Zhang,Binghao Han,Linlin Ding,Yue Kou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于对比学习的动态多模态数据融合模型（CL-DMDF），通过跨特征和模态维度的注意力机制、实体质心对比学习以及自适应融合模块，有效应对实际应用中模态缺失和不确定性问题，在三个数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化多模态融合方法通常针对特定任务设计并假设模态完整，但实际应用中常存在模态缺失或不确定性问题；同时，传统模型过度关注缺失模态内的局部交互，忽略了多模态表示中蕴含的全局互补线索。

Method: 1）提出跨特征维度和模态维度的新型注意力机制，计算可靠注意力分数以反映各层级重要性；2）引入实体质心对比学习模块，基于实体特征构建质心正样本以增强判别学习；3）采用自适应融合模块提升动态融合策略的效率与准确性。

Result: 在三个数据集上的广泛实验表明，CL-DMDF在多种多模态融合任务中均展现了有效性。

Conclusion: CL-DMDF通过注意力机制、质心对比学习和自适应融合的联合设计，有效解决了模态缺失场景下的多模态融合问题，为动态多模态数据融合提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal data fusion involves integrating and analyzing information from multiple modalities to uncover latent correlations and complementary patterns, thereby enhancing data processing and decision-making. While existing methods for structured multimodal inputs are typically designed around specific tasks and assume fully observed modalities, real-world applications often suffer from uncertain or missing modality inputs due to various factors. Some traditional models overly emphasize local interactions within missing modalities, neglecting the global complementary cues embedded in multimodal representations. To overcome these limitations, we propose a Dynamic Multimodal Data Fusion model based on Contrastive Learning (CL-DMDF). CL-DMDF introduces a novel attention mechanism that operates across both feature and modality dimensions to compute reliable attention scores, effectively reflecting importance at each level. The CL-DMDF further incorporates an entity-centroid contrastive learning module that constructs centroid-based positive samples from entity features to enhance discriminative learning. Additionally, an adaptive fusion module is employed to improve the efficiency and accuracy of dynamic fusion strategies. Extensive experiments conducted on three datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the CL-DMDF across diverse multimodal fusion tasks.

</details>


### [100] [Neural Networks Provably Learn Spectral Representations for Group Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02993)
*Jianliang He,Leda Wang,Fengzhuo Zhang,Siyu Chen,Zhuoran Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过谱域投影梯度流分析了两层神经网络在群复合任务上的训练动力学，证明神经元几乎必然收敛到单一不可约表示，并揭示了表示竞争、低秩压缩和相位对齐等现象，为特征学习提供了表示论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络训练中结构化内部表征如何涌现是深度学习研究的核心问题，本文旨在从表示论角度揭示这一机制。

Method: 将投影梯度流提升到傅里叶域，将训练动力学建模为表示论能量泛函上的黎曼梯度上升，并利用随机初始化进行理论分析。

Result: 1) 神经元几乎必然收敛到单一不可约表示；2) 跨层傅里叶系数达到旋转秩一对齐；3) 对Abel群，随机初始化导致均匀多样化及Haar均匀相位；4) 相位对齐与表示竞争以指数收敛速度出现。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了特征学习的表示论理论，揭示了群表示的矩阵值低秩压缩新现象，并通过指数收敛速度证明了表示竞争和相位对齐的必然性。

Abstract: Understanding how structured internal structure emerges during neural network training is central to the study of deep learning. We investigate this phenomenon through the group composition task, where a two-layer neural network is trained to predict $g_1 \star g_2$ for elements of a finite group $G$. By lifting the projected gradient flow to the Fourier domain, we demonstrate that the training dynamics are governed by a Riemannian gradient ascent on a representation-theoretic energy functional. We prove that, under random initialization, this flow drives each neuron to converge almost surely toward a single irreducible representation, while the cross-layer Fourier coefficients achieve a rotational rank-one alignment. This framework provides a representation-theoretic account of feature learning and characterizes a novel low-rank compression phenomenon for matrix-valued group representations. Moreover, for Abelian groups, we provide a complete population-level description: random initialization promotes uniform diversification across nontrivial representations and induces Haar-uniform phases, jointly approximating the indicator via a majority-vote mechanism. We further prove that both phase alignment and representation competition emerge with exponential convergence rates.

</details>


### [101] [Improvise, Adapt, Overcome: An On-The-Fly Multifidelity Algorithm for Efficient Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02662)
*Vivin Vinod,Peter Zaspel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应多保真度机器学习框架（adaptive-MFML），通过动态查询各保真度训练样本，自动确定训练数据集组成，在量子化学计算中相比单保真度方法最高降低30倍数据生成成本，相比标准MFML提升最高5倍效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习加速量子化学面临高保真训练数据生成成本过高的问题，而现有标准多保真度方法依赖预定义的缩放因子来确定数据比例，容易产生冗余数据导致效率损失。

Method: 提出自适应在线多保真度框架，动态查询各保真度训练样本，算法先在低保真度上饱和模型精度，再自动向更昂贵的参考计算迁移，自主决定训练数据集组成。

Result: 在耦合簇能量和激发能等化学性质上，自适应算法相比单保真度方法降低数据生成成本达30倍，相比标准MFML提升达5倍。

Conclusion: 自适应多保真度方法通过缓解数据冗余，建立了高精度低成本可持续的量子化学机器学习路径。

Abstract: Machine learning has accelerated quantum chemistry but is hindered by the prohibitive cost of generating high fidelity training data. Multifidelity machine learning (MFML) mitigates this overhead by systematically combining abundant low fidelity data with sparse high fidelity data. In spite of its success, standard MFML schemes rely on pre-defined scaling factors to determine sparse data ratio across fidelities, often generating redundant multifidelity data resulting in a loss of efficiency. Here, we introduce an adaptive on-the-fly multifidelity framework for machine learning that autonomously determines training dataset composition. By dynamically querying training samples at each fidelity, the algorithm saturates model accuracy at lower fidelities before moving up to more expensive reference calculations. We benchmark the novel adaptive-MFML across diverse chemical properties including the computational chemistry gold standard coupled cluster energies, and the more chemically challenging excitation energies. In our numerical experiments we show that our adaptive algorithm reduces data generation costs by up to a factor of 30 compared to single fidelity methods and improves upon standard MFML by up to a factor of 5. The mitigation of data redundancy establishes a high-accuracy low-cost pathway for sustainable cost-aware machine learning in quantum chemistry.

</details>


### [102] [AdaWeather: Adaptively Mixing Probabilistic Weather Forecasts with Logarithmic Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02663)
*Saptarishi Dhanuka,Sarvesh Iyer,Manmeet Singh,Mihir More,Rushil Gupta,Dhruman Gupta,Parthasarathi Mukhopadhyay,Sandeep Juneja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaWeather是一个自适应框架，通过结合机器学习和专家混合方法，整合多个概率天气预报模型，实现对最佳静态专家混合体的对数遗憾界，在温度预测任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率天气预报模型在时空上不能一致地主导，相对性能高度依赖上下文，需要自适应方法组合多种预报以获得改进和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出AdaWeather框架，将多个概率预报通过机器学习与专家混合相结合，扩展了传统专家方法的遗憾分析，证明算法相对于最佳静态专家混合体具有对数遗憾界。

Result: 在温度预测任务上，AdaWeather相比现有方法取得了改进。

Conclusion: AdaWeather为组合多种概率天气预报提供了一种自适应的、理论上保证遗憾界的方法，并实证了其有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in machine learning have produced probabilistic weather forecasting models comparable to state-of-the-art numerical weather predictors. But no model consistently dominates spatio-temporally, and relative performance is highly context-dependent. This motivates adaptive methods for combining multiple forecasts to obtain improvements and robustness. While combined forecasts have been proposed in the literature, these are achieved either through supervised learning or through prediction with expert advice methods. We introduce AdaWeather, an adaptive framework that combines many probabilistic forecasts using both machine learning as well as mixture of experts to arrive at a unified improved probabilistic forecast. While traditional expert methods develop the regret bounds with respect to the best single expert in hindsight, we extend the algorithm and analysis to show our method has logarithmic regret compared to the best static mixture of experts in hindsight. Empirically, we focus on forecasting temperature, and observe improvements over existing methods.

</details>


### [103] [Analytical Evaluation of DCA Convergence Properties for Minimizing Prediction Functions of Gaussian RBF Support Vector Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03559)
*Yohei Kakimoto,Yuto Omae,Hirotaka Takahashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对基于RBF核的SVR模型的非凸优化问题，提出了一种利用RBF核解析结构构建显式DC分解的DCA框架，导出了强凸性下界μ和梯度Lipschitz常数上界L的闭式表达式，两者共享主导项C_αρ。实验表明C_αρ是刻画DCA收敛性和初始点依赖性的主要单一量，且可在训练前后通过该标量预先评估收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于以训练好的RBF-SVR模型预测函数为目标的非凸优化问题，现有方法缺乏有效的处理框架，需要构建适用于DCA的显式DC分解并量化其收敛性质。

Method: 利用RBF核的解析结构构建显式DC分解，推导DC分量强凸性下界μ和子问题梯度Lipschitz常数上界L的闭式表达式（均由C_α、γ和ρ决定，共享主导项C_αρ），并通过六个基准函数实验验证。

Result: C_αρ是刻画DCA收敛性质和初始点依赖性的主要单一量，可分解为C→C_α和γ→ρ两条独立路径，其主要变化由SVR超参数(C,γ)控制。

Conclusion: 通过单一标量C_αρ可在训练前（近似）和训练后（精确闭式）预先评估DCA在RBF-SVR上的收敛性能。

Abstract: For nonconvex optimization problems whose objective is the prediction function of a trained Support Vector Regression (SVR) model with the Gaussian radial basis function (RBF) kernel (RBF-SVR), we present a framework that applies the difference of convex functions (DC) algorithm (DCA) by exploiting the analytical structure of the RBF kernel to construct an explicit DC decomposition. Specifically, we derive in closed form both the lower bound $μ$ of the strong convexity parameter of the DC components and the upper bound $L$ of the gradient Lipschitz constant of the subproblem. Both $μ$ and $L$ are determined solely by the post-training dual-coefficient sum $C_α$ and the RBF kernel parameter $γ$, together with the DC decomposition parameter $ρ$, and they share a common leading term $C_αρ$. Through numerical experiments on six benchmark functions, we show that $C_αρ$ is the primary single quantity characterizing both the convergence properties and the initial-point dependence of DCA, and further demonstrate that it decomposes into two independent pathways, $C \to C_α$ and $γ\to ρ$, with its primary variation governed by the SVR hyperparameters $(C, γ)$. Together, these results allow the convergence properties of DCA on RBF-SVR to be assessed in advance through the single scalar quantity $C_αρ$: approximately from $(C, γ)$ before training, and exactly in closed form after training.

</details>


### [104] [Anomalies in Multivariate Time Series Benchmarks Are Mostly Univariate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02670)
*Marc Pinet,Julien Cumin,Samuel Berlemont,Dominique Vaufreydaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 当前多元时间序列异常检测基准中，跨通道建模并未带来实际性能提升，因为几乎所有标注异常都存在单变量偏差，而跨通道结构变化极少单独出现。现有基准不适合验证跨通道建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多MT-SAD模型假设异常结构可能跨越多个通道，因此引入跨通道建模。但该假设是否成立？作者旨在系统评估这一假说。

Method: 提出逐段诊断框架：对每个标注异常段，检测是否至少一个通道存在单变量偏差、跨通道相关性是否改变，或两者兼有。同时构建合成数据（相位偏移正弦通道+共享噪声）作为对照验证。

Result: 1) 八个基准中无跨通道破裂单独发生，所有跨通道异常均伴随单变量偏差；2) 六个基准中至少半数异常段在79%-100%时间步上存在单变量偏差；3) 合成数据上，CD模型有效而CI模型失败，验证了框架能正确捕获跨通道结构；4) 真实基准上CD与CI模型性能无显著差异。

Conclusion: 当前MTSAD基准不适合验证跨通道建模能力，需开发结构更多样化的评估数据集。

Abstract: Many recent multivariate time series anomaly detection (MT-SAD) models incorporate cross-channel modeling, under the implicit assumption that the structure of anomalies may be spread across multiple channels. We evaluate this assumption on eight widely used public benchmarks by introducing a per-segment diagnostic framework that flags, for each labeled anomaly, whether at least one channel deviates individually from its normal history, whether the cross-channel correlation structure changes, or both. The framework shows that no crosschannel rupture occurs without an accompanying univariate deviation across a range of reasonable thresholds. A complementary metric also reveals that on six of the eight benchmarks, at least half of the labeled anomaly segments deviate univariately on 79% to 100% of their timesteps, reaching 100% on three of these datasets. To verify that our framework captures cross-channel structure when present, we construct synthetic data of phase-shifted sinusoidal channels with shared noise. Each anomalous segment is altered through one of two channelwise corruptions that preserve the per-channel marginal distribution while breaking cross-channel structure, and our framework correctly characterizes these segments as cross-channel-only. On these data, channel-dependent (CD) models successfully exploit the cross-channel signal whereas channel-independent (CI) ones fail. The CI/CD comparison of a recent SOTA detector on real benchmarks further confirms that CD modeling brings no measurable gain. We conclude that current MTSAD benchmarks are unsuitable for validating cross-channel modeling capabilities, and we call for the development of more structurally diverse evaluation sets. The code for this study is publicly available.

</details>


### [105] [AugMask: Training Diffusion Models on Incomplete Tabular Data via Stochastic Augmentation and Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03347)
*Jungkyu Kim,Taeyoung Park,Kibok Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AugMask框架，通过条件随机增强和仅对观测坐标去噪监督，使缺失不感知的扩散模型骨干网络适应表格数据中的缺失值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于得分的扩散模型在表格数据应用中面临挑战，因为真实表格数据常含有缺失值，而主流骨干网络要求完全指定的输入。

Method: 1) 使用轻量辅助模型进行条件随机增强构建数值输入；2) 仅对观测坐标施加去噪监督。将缺失条目标视为不确定的条件上下文而非训练目标。该方法与Rao-Blackwellized目标函数相关联，缺失条目的边际化产生方差加权敏感性惩罚，抑制对不确定补全的过度依赖。

Result: 在多种数据集和缺失率设定下，AugMask使标准扩散表格生成器超越专门处理缺失值的基线方法。

Conclusion: AugMask作为一种即插即用的训练框架，有效解决了扩散模型处理表格数据缺失值的问题，通过分离条件与监督实现了更鲁棒的生成。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models have emerged as prominent deep generative models; however, their application to tabular data remains challenging because their backbones assume fully specified inputs, whereas real-world tabular data often contain missing values. We propose AugMask, a plug-and-play training framework that adapts missing-unaware backbones to incomplete data by separating conditioning from supervision. AugMask 1) constructs numeric inputs via conditional stochastic augmentation using lightweight auxiliary models, and 2) applies denoising supervision only to observed coordinates. In effect, augmented missing entries serve as uncertain conditioning context rather than training targets. We connect this training rule to a Rao--Blackwellized objective and show that marginalizing missing entries yields a variance-weighted sensitivity penalty, discouraging over-reliance on uncertain completions. Across diverse datasets and missingness regimes, AugMask enables standard diffusion-based tabular generators to outperform specialized missing-aware baselines.

</details>


### [106] [Aligning Data-Driven Predictors with Allocation: A Decision-Focused Approach to Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02671)
*Itai Zilberstein,Ioannis Anagnostides,Tuomas Sandholm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出传统生存预测模型（如基于C-index优化）在器官分配等决策任务中可能导致任意差的分配效果，并提出基于NDCG的决策导向学习框架，在历史心脏移植数据上使NDCG提升50-100%，每年可增加数万生命年。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习预测模型通常独立于下游算法任务进行优化（如仅优化C-index等统计指标），导致预测精度与决策效用之间存在严重错位。在高风险器官分配场景中，即使使用高精度生存预测模型，其分配效果可能差于随机选择。

Method: 提出决策导向的生存分析学习框架：1) 引入信息检索中的NDCG作为优化目标，并证明NDCG优化可转化为分配效用的保证；2) 提出基于自举法（bootstrapping）优化现有生存模型NDCG的方法；3) 解决右删失数据对排序评估的挑战。

Result: 在美国历史心脏移植数据上，该方法将基线模型的NDCG提升50-100%，部署于移植分配时可转化为每年数万额外生命年。

Conclusion: 决策导向的NDCG优化能有效弥合生存分析与策略优化之间的鸿沟，有望在更广泛的预测驱动决策场景中得到应用。

Abstract: Machine learning predictors have become essential tools for guiding automated decision making. However, a major misalignment persists: predictive models are typically optimized in terms of standard statistical metrics in isolation from the algorithmic tasks they inform. We highlight this incongruity in the high-stakes domain of organ allocation by demonstrating that any algorithm relying on (even highly accurate) survival predictors optimized for standard metrics -- such as the Concordance index (C-index) -- can yield arbitrarily poor outcomes when used for allocation, failing to guarantee utility better than a uniform random selection. To bridge the gap between survival analysis and policy optimization, we introduce a decision-focused learning approach based on optimizing normalized discounted cumulative gain (NDCG), a mainstay metric in information retrieval. We establish the utility of NDCG in survival analysis by proving that it translates to guarantees on the performance of allocation. Empirically, we propose a bootstrapping approach to optimize the NDCG of existing survival models. Unlike prior work, we also address the challenge of right censorship when evaluating ranking. On historical heart transplant data from the US, our method dramatically boosts the NDCG of baseline models by 50-100%, which translates to tens of thousands of additional life years gained annually when deployed for transplant allocation. We anticipate that our framework will find broader applications in decision making with predictions.

</details>


### [107] [Before Fusion, Ask What to Keep: Contextual Calibration of Multimodal Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02679)
*Jiyuan Liu,Liangwei Nathan Zheng,Wei Emma Zhang,Xinpei Wang,Weitong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级校准模块，在多模态融合前对各模态特征进行实例级和维度级调制，通过提取跨模态的支持/冲突信号来抑制误导性信息、保留弱证据，适用于多种融合骨干网络，在五个基准任务上提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态系统虽然通常受益于跨语言、声音和视觉信息的整合，但这种收益并不保证——某些模态对特定输入可能反而造成干扰，且同一模态内的局部特征响应可能与其他模态的证据相矛盾。因此需要一种方法在多模态表示融合前对其进行调整。

Method: 设计了一个紧凑的校准模块，以摘要级别比较各模态，提取跨源支持和冲突线索，将其转化为实例级和维度级的调制信号。校准直接应用于原始模态特征（而非已融合的表示），从而抑制误导成分、保留弱证据、强调受多模态上下文支持的响应。该模块作为即插即用组件，可附加到不同融合骨干网络上而不改变其预测头。

Result: 在情感理解、动作识别、音视频事件检测和音视频情感分类五个基准上，所提出的融合前校准策略在基于序列和基于卷积的融合设置下均提升了性能。消融实验、合成损坏、训练动态和特征可视化分析表明，融合前校准信号能减少不可靠模态的干扰，产生更稳定的多模态优化。

Conclusion: 融合前对多模态特征进行校准是一种有效的策略，能够通过跨模态比较生成调制信号来改善融合质量，且作为即插即用模块具备良好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Multimodal systems often benefit from combining information across language, sound, and visual streams, but this benefit is not guaranteed. A modality that is useful for one input may become distracting for another, and local feature responses within the same modality can disagree with evidence from other sources. This work investigates how to adjust multimodal representations before they are merged by a downstream predictor. We develop a compact calibration module that compares each modality with the others at the summary level, extracts cues of cross-source support and conflict, and converts these cues into instance-wise and dimension-wise modulation signals. The calibration is applied to the original modality features rather than to already fused representations, enabling the model to suppress misleading components, preserve weak but useful evidence, and emphasize responses that are better supported by the current multimodal context. The module is designed as a plug-in component and can be attached to different fusion backbones without changing their prediction heads. Across five benchmarks covering sentiment understanding, action recognition, audio-visual event detection, and audio-visual emotion classification, the proposed pre-combination calibration strategy improves performance under both sequence-based and convolutional fusion settings. Additional analyses under modality removal, synthetic corruption, training dynamics, and feature-level visualization show that calibrating signals before fusion can reduce interference from unreliable modalities and produce more stable multimodal optimization.

</details>


### [108] [Conformal Language Modeling via Posterior Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03731)
*Nicolas Emmenegger,Theo X. Olausson,Armando Solar-Lezama,Chara Podimata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过近似LLM后验分布进行采样的方法，在条件序列生成中实现校准，相比事后纠错方法，在保持统计保证的同时提升了生成质量与实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于共形预测的事后滤波方法将采样过程视为原子操作，生成后再手术式去除幻觉，导致样本不连贯、不一致，且无法将概率质量转移到更有用的回答上。

Method: 从LLM后验分布的近似中进行采样，其中条件事件对应校准的高分区域；开发了适用于条件序列生成设置的校准程序，有效识别该区域并实现目标风险控制。

Result: 在开放传记生成和数学问题求解案例中，与先前工作相比，获得相同的统计保证，且下游实用性更高。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持统计风险控制的同时，克服了事后滤波导致的连贯性下降和概率分配不当问题，提升了生成质量。

Abstract: Large Language Models remain plagued by hallucinations. Recent work has sought to tame their prevalence using statistical techniques based on conformal prediction, with both theoretical and empirical success. However, these methods operate in a post-hoc fashion, treating the sampling procedure itself as atomic and then surgically altering samples to remove hallucinated claims. This disconnect between filtering and generation can result in samples that are incoherent, inconsistent, or simply unlikely under the model itself. Moreover, post-hoc surgery is unable to shift probability mass towards more useful and helpful responses. To address these issues, we propose to instead sample from approximations to an LLM posterior, where the conditioning event corresponds to a calibrated, high-scoring region. We develop a calibration procedure tailored to the setting of conditional sequential generation that effectively identifies this region and achieves target risk control. Empirically, we apply our method to case studies focused on open-ended biography generation and mathematical problem solving; compared to prior work, we obtain the same statistical guarantees, with higher downstream utility.

</details>


### [109] [Locality Does Not Imply Reachability: Boundary Repair in Block-Sparse Causal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02680)
*Zhibo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀疏因果注意力中序列局部性与注意力图可达性之间的不匹配问题。在固定块因果注意力中，相邻token可能在注意力图中被完全断开连接。通过结构依赖集形式化这一边界伪影，推导了可达性的相位条件覆盖函数，并提出边界桥接注意力（Boundary Bridge Attention）作为零参数修复方案。实验验证了覆盖理论对稀疏模式失败与修复效果的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏因果注意力通常基于序列局部性假设（近处token易访问，远处token可丢弃），但本文发现该假设与注意力图的实际可达性存在系统性不匹配：固定块因果掩码在块边界处会切断相邻token的注意力路径，导致模型在学习跨块依赖时存在理论上的性能上界（top-1准确率上限1/K，交叉熵下界log K）。

Method: 1) 形式化结构依赖集，证明固定块因果注意力中目标表示仅能依赖于自身块前缀内的token；2) 推导相位条件覆盖函数，揭示可达性同时取决于源-目标距离和目标在块内的偏移量；3) 提出边界桥接注意力（Boundary Bridge Attention），在保持固定块路径不变的同时，通过共享投影在块边界处添加零额外参数的辅助因果边；4) 进行1024 token控制实验和8K token Qwen2.5-7B探针实验验证。

Result: 1) 理论推导显示固定块因果注意力在K路边界-复制分布上存在架构级性能上限（top-1准确率≤1/K，交叉熵≥log K）；2) 覆盖函数准确预测了稀疏模式的失败条件、修复方案的有效性，以及滑动窗口注意力与边界修复的不可互换性；3) 1024 token实验显示边界桥接注意力的增益集中在覆盖对齐的诊断指标上；4) 8K token Qwen2.5-7B探针实验复现了覆盖不可比性模式，提供了外部有效性证据。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了一个理论指导的诊断框架，用于分析块稀疏因果注意力中的局部性-可达性不匹配问题，包括相位条件覆盖分析和最小构造性修复方案。边界桥接注意力作为建设性证据，证明了在零额外参数条件下修复边界伪影的可行性。

Abstract: Sparse causal attention is usually described by sequence locality: nearby tokens should remain easy to access, while distant tokens may be dropped to reduce cost. This paper studies a mismatch between sequence locality and attention-graph reachability. In fixed block causal attention, two adjacent tokens can be disconnected in the attention graph at every depth. We formalize this boundary artifact through structural dependency sets: if every attention layer uses the same fixed block causal mask and all remaining operations are positionwise, a target representation can depend only on tokens in its own block prefix. This yields an architecture-level boundary-copy separation for a constructed K-way boundary-copy distribution, with top-1 accuracy upper bound 1/K and expected cross-entropy lower bound log K. We then derive phase-conditioned coverage functions showing that reachability depends on both source-target distance and the target's offset within its block. These coverage laws predict when a sparse pattern should fail, when a repair can help, and why sliding-window attention and boundary repair are not interchangeable. Boundary Bridge Attention is treated as a constructive witness: it preserves the fixed block path and adds zero-additional-parameter auxiliary causal edges near block boundaries using shared projections. Controlled 1024-token experiments show that gains concentrate in coverage-aligned diagnostics. As secondary external-validity evidence, a fixed-checkpoint 8K-token Qwen2.5-7B probe shows the same coverage-incomparability pattern. The contribution is a theory-guided diagnostic framework for locality-reachability mismatch in block-sparse causal attention, together with phase-conditioned coverage analysis and a minimal constructive repair.

</details>


### [110] [Online Learning with Gradient-Variation Interval Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03831)
*Yan-Feng Xie,Shuche Wang,Peng Zhao,Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于梯度变化量（gradient variation）实现区间遗憾（interval regret）上界的在线学习算法，采用双层在线集成结构，能同时自适应多种问题相关量并保持最小最大最优速率，还提出了无需预知Lipschitz/光滑度参数的变体，并在区间动态遗憾和随机扩展对抗优化中具有更广泛的意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非平稳在线学习算法在区间遗憾度量下缺乏能够随梯度变化量（衡量在线函数梯度累积变化的根本性指标）缩放的理论保证，而梯度变化量与多种问题相关量及随机优化等问题紧密相连，因此需要设计一种能自适应该度量的算法。

Method: 提出一个简单高效的双层在线集成结构（two-layer online ensemble structure）；为应对超参数调优挑战，引入一个新颖的Lipschitz自适应元算法（Lipschitz-adaptive meta algorithm），从而实现不依赖Lipschitz和光滑度先验知识的变体。

Result: 算法在区间遗憾上达到了随梯度变化量缩放的上界，同时保持了最坏情况下的最小最大最优速率；在区间动态遗憾和随机扩展对抗优化上获得了更通用的界；实验结果验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于梯度变化量的非平稳在线学习提供了首个区间遗憾保证，其Lipschitz自适应元算法具有独立价值，并在多个更广泛的度量上取得了突破性理论结果。

Abstract: This paper investigates non-stationary online learning using the metric of interval regret, which requires an online algorithm to perform well over every time interval. We propose the first online learning algorithm that achieves an interval regret bound scaling with gradient variation, a fundamental measure of the cumulative change in online function gradients, which relates to various problem-dependent quantities and is closely connected to stochastic optimization and other problems. Our method employs a simple and efficient two-layer online ensemble structure that achieves strong theoretical guarantees. Specifically, it enjoys a regret bound that simultaneously adapts to various problem-dependent quantities while also preserving the minimax-optimal rate in the worst case. Moreover, recognizing the challenge of hyperparameter tuning, we introduce a Lipschitz- and smoothness-agnostic variant that automatically adapts to these potentially unknown constants. This is primarily enabled by a novel Lipschitz-adaptive meta algorithm, which may be of independent interest. Beyond interval regret, our method also yields broader implications: it provides versatile bounds for interval dynamic regret, a stronger measure that competes with changing comparators over any interval, and yields the first piecewise characterization for stochastic extended adversarial optimization. Theoretical findings are validated by experiments.

</details>


### [111] [Filter, Then Reweight: Rethinking Optimization Granularity in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02684)
*Yuying Li,Leqi Zheng,Yongzi Yu,Wenrui Zhou,Xuchang Zhong,Xing Hu,Jing Jin,Huangjie Yuan,Tao Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FiRe-OPD方法，通过"先过滤轨迹再重新加权token"的两阶段策略，在轨迹和token两个层面联合优化师生蒸馏中的监督信号，实现更细粒度的ON-Policy蒸馏，在多种设置下超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ON-Policy蒸馏方法正从全轨迹KL监督转向选择性训练范式，但现有方法仅在轨迹或token单一层面进行选择，缺乏联合优化。作者希望通过同时调整轨迹和token两个粒度的监督信号，实现更精细的蒸馏优化。

Method: FiRe-OPD采用两阶段策略：第一阶段过滤低质量轨迹（剔除劣质rollout样本），第二阶段在保留轨迹内对token进行软重新加权（soft reweighting），以突出信息性强的token。与硬选择不同，软加权机制可减少信息丢失并增强优化稳定性。

Result: 在强到弱、单教师、多教师三种设置下均验证有效性。在强到弱设置中，AIME 2024提升6.25；在多教师设置中，Miner提升18.81，优于近期token级OPD方法。

Conclusion: FiRe-OPD通过轨迹过滤+token软加权的联合优化策略，实现了更细粒度的ON-Policy蒸馏，有效平衡了选择性训练与信息保留，在多个基准上取得显著提升。

Abstract: On-Policy distillation (OPD) in large language models is shifting from full-trace KL supervision toward more selective training paradigms. Recent OPD methods increasingly focus on selecting which trajectories to learn from, which tokens are most informative, and which supervision signals are most reliable. Motivated by this trend, we rethink optimization granularity of OPD and propose \fireicon\ FiRe-OPD (Filter, then Reweight), which jointly adjusts supervision signals at both trajectory and token levels. In details, FiRe-OPD first filters trajectories to remove low-quality rollout samples, and then applies soft reweighting within the retained trajectories to emphasize informative tokens. Compared with hard token selection, FiRe-OPD leverages a soft-weighting mechanism to effectively mitigate information loss and enhance optimization stability, thereby achieving finer-grained OPD optimization.
