For self-adjoint groups, Bürgisser, Franks, Garg, Oliveira, Walter and Wigderson gave a geodesic optimization algorithm whose complexity is controlled by the gap, a condition number of the representation. We study the gap for quiver representations under the action of the special linear group.
  We prove that the inverse gap is polynomially bounded in the number of vertices and the maximum dimension for type A and $\hat{A}$, as well as tree quivers with uniform dimension vectors. Consequently, the algorithm of Bürgisser et al. solves the nullcone membership problem in polynomial time for these families. In contrast, we construct families of quivers and dimension vectors where the gap is exponentially small in the number of leaves, furthermore, for every connected quiver we exhibit dimension vectors such that the weight margin (a related condition number) is exponentially small in the number of vertices.
  We also extend our results to $σ$-semistability, thereby giving a new proof of a recent result of Iwamasa, Oki, and Soma.

</details>


### [232] [Disciplined Nonlinear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02896)
*Daniel Cederberg,William Zhang,Parth Nobel,Stephen Boyd*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“规整非线性规划（DNLP）”的语法规范，用于描述非线性规划问题。DNLP受规整凸规划（DCP）启发，允许将光滑函数与非光滑凸/凹函数自由混合，并通过规则约束非光滑函数的使用。DNLP形式的问题可自动规范化到标准非线性规划形式，并传递给合适的NLP求解器。该规范化以无损方式松弛非光滑凸/凹函数，使其能被要求光滑函数的NLP求解器处理。此外，将原问题转化为等效NLP形式带来了若干优势，包括更简单的问题初始化。本文描述了该语言及其作为CVXPY扩展的开源实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性规划求解器通常要求目标函数和约束条件光滑可微，限制了非光滑凸/凹函数的直接使用；而规整凸规划（DCP）虽然能处理非光滑凸函数，但局限于凸优化问题。因此需要一种既能处理非光滑函数又能保留NLP灵活性的语法框架。

Method: 受DCP启发设计DNLP语法，允许光滑函数与非光滑凸/凹函数混合使用，并通过语法规则约束非光滑函数的位置。通过自动化规范化（canonicalization）将DNLP问题无损转化为等效的NLP形式，利用松弛技术处理非光滑函数，最终传递给标准NLP求解器。基于CVXPY实现开源扩展。

Result: 成功设计并实现DNLP语法及其CVXPY扩展，使非光滑凸/凹函数能被标准NLP求解器处理，同时简化问题初始化等过程。

Conclusion: DNLP扩展了传统NLP的适用范围，使其能够处理有用的非光滑凸/凹函数，同时保持NLP的通用性和求解器兼容性。

Abstract: We introduce disciplined nonlinear programming (DNLP), a syntax for specifying nonlinear programming problems. DNLP is inspired by disciplined convex programming (DCP) and allows smooth functions to be freely mixed with nonsmooth convex and concave functions, with rules governing how the nonsmooth functions can be used. Problems expressed in DNLP form can be automatically canonicalized to a standard nonlinear programming (NLP) form and passed to a suitable NLP solver. As in DCP, the canonicalization relaxes nonsmooth convex and concave functions in a lossless way, allowing them to be handled by NLP solvers that require smooth functions. In addition to extending NLP to include useful nondifferentiable convex and concave functions, transforming the original problem to an equivalent NLP form offers several advantages, including simpler problem initialization. We describe the language and our open-source implementation of DNLP as an extension of CVXPY, a parser for DCP.

</details>


### [233] [One-Shot Optimization with Additional Inequality Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02925)
*Lea Fischer,Nicolas R. Gauger,Lisa Kusch*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将单次优化框架扩展至含不等式约束的优化问题，通过松弛变量嵌入双重增广拉格朗日函数，得到含非光滑主动集依赖项的系统，并分析广义Hessian的正定性条件以确保严格局部最优性，同时展望了基于广义Hessian近似的预条件子构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单次优化框架主要针对等式约束问题，本文旨在将其扩展至更一般的不等式约束情形，以处理由计算昂贵的稳态系统控制的设计任务。

Method: 采用松弛变量将不等式约束转化为等式约束，并嵌入双重增广拉格朗日函数；消去松弛变量后得到含非光滑主动集依赖项的耦合单次矩阵结构；利用Clarke和Rockafellar–Wets广义二阶次微分分析非光滑主动集转换；推导广义Hessian正定性的显式条件。

Result: 得到了形如∇DLag = -Ms的广义梯度系统表示；推导了确保所有可容许广义Hessian正定性的显式正性条件，从而保证严格局部最优性；给出了基于广义Hessian近似的预条件子构造思路。

Conclusion: 成功将单次优化框架从等式约束扩展至不等式约束，为非光滑主动集转换提供了严格的理论分析，并为大规模优化问题的高效求解奠定了预条件子基础。

Abstract: The one-shot approach is a powerful simultaneous optimization framework for design tasks governed by computationally expensive steady-state systems. While previous formulations mainly focused on additional equality constraints, this work extends the one-shot framework to optimization problems with inequality constraints using slack variables embedded into a doubly augmented Lagrangian formulation.
  After elimination of the slack variables, the resulting formulation contains nonsmooth active-set dependent terms while preserving the characteristic coupled one-shot matrix structure. The resulting generalized gradient system admits a representation of the form $
\nabla \DLag = -Ms, $
analogous to the equality-constrained one-shot framework.
  To analyze the nonsmooth active-set transitions, generalized second-order subdifferentials in the sense of Clarke and Rockafellar--Wets are employed. We derive explicit positivity conditions ensuring positive definiteness of all admissible generalized Hessians and hence strict local optimality. Furthermore, we give an outlook on the construction of preconditioners based on generalized Hessian approximations.

</details>


### [234] [Koopman operator learning for predictive control via Khatri-Rao kernel regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02938)
*Mircea Lazar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的广义Koopman算子（GeKo）实现方法，通过独立提升状态和输入、构建张量双线性系统，结合多步Khatri-Rao核回归和结构化SVD降维，在混沌Lorenz系统上验证了其稳定不稳定平衡点的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Koopman算子方法在处理带控制输入的非线性系统时，往往只利用单步快照对进行学习，导致累积预测误差较大；同时缺乏有效的降维手段来平衡模型复杂度与预测精度。

Method: 1) 时间序列多步Khatri-Rao核回归：利用轨迹上的多步演化快照而非单步配对来学习算子，降低累积预测误差；2) 结构化SVD降维：对提升后的状态和输入空间进行核无关和输入无关的压缩，同时保持Khatri-Rao实现结构；3) 采用随机傅里叶特征实例化框架，并构建完整的预测控制流程，包含引导MPC时域选择的多步展开诊断。

Result: 在混沌Lorenz系统上，学习到的降阶GeKo模型能够在不同初始条件下稳定系统的非稳定平衡点。

Conclusion: 本文提出的GeKo框架结合多步学习与结构化降维，有效提升了数据驱动Koopman算子的预测控制性能，尤其在混沌系统中展现出良好的稳定能力。

Abstract: This paper develops a data-driven realization of the generalized Koopman operator (GeKo), in which states and inputs are lifted independently and the dynamics are expressed as a tensor bilinear system. The first contribution is a time-sequenced multi-step Khatri-Rao kernel regression formulation that exposes the operator to evolved snapshots along trajectories rather than only single one-step pairs, which reduces compounded prediction error. Secondly, we develop a kernel- and input-agnostic structured SVD reduction that compresses the lifted state and input spaces while preserving the Khatri-Rao realization. We instantiate the framework with random Fourier features and describe a complete predictive-control pipeline, including a multi-step roll-out diagnostic that guides the choice of MPC horizon. The framework is validated on the chaotic Lorenz system, where the learned reduced-order GeKo model stabilizes an unstable equilibrium from a range of initial conditions.

</details>


### [235] [The Value Function Semi-Algebraic Set in Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03048)
*Ryan A. Anderson,Guido Montufar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文刻画了无限时域部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）在无记忆随机策略下可行值函数集合的半代数结构，证明了该集合由显式多项式不等式定义，揭示了部分可观测性导致的非线性约束及其对策略优化的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将完全可观测MDP中可行值函数为多面体的经典结果推广到更复杂的部分可观测场景，理解POMDP下可行值函数的几何结构。

Method: 通过半代数几何方法，利用转移动力学、观测核和奖励结构构造显式多项式不等式，刻画POMDP可行值函数集合。

Result: POMDP可行值函数集合是半代数集（而非MDP中的多面体），由多项式不等式定义，存在孤立局部极大值，且依赖于初始状态分布。

Conclusion: 部分可观测性导致非线性约束，使值函数几何结构更丰富复杂，为MDP和POMDP策略优化提供新见解。

Abstract: We study the geometry of feasible value functions in infinite-horizon partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) under memoryless stochastic policies. Our main contribution is a characterization of the feasible set of value functions as a semi-algebraic set, defined by explicit polynomial inequalities determined by the transition dynamics, observation kernel, and reward structure of the POMDP. This result extends prior work for fully observable Markov decision processes, where the feasible set is known to be a polytope, to the substantially more intricate partially observable setting. In contrast to the polyhedral structure arising in MDPs, partial observability induces fundamentally nonlinear constraints, leading to a richer and more complex geometric structure. Our geometric characterization provides new insight into the landscape of policy optimization in both MDPs and POMDPs, and reveals qualitative phenomena unique to partial observability, including the emergence of isolated local maximizers of the long-term reward and their dependence on the initial state distribution.

</details>


### [236] [A Less Conservative Sufficient Condition for PID Stabilization of Scalar Second-Order Nonlinear Uncertain Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03213)
*Senhan Yao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对标量二阶非线性不确定系统的鲁棒设定点调节问题，提出了一种端点平衡的二次加积分Lyapunov证书，通过平衡混合项惩罚来降低保守性，使得固定增益PID控制器能够保证全局渐近调节，且认证的PID区域严格包含现有方法的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Lyapunov充分条件认证的固定增益PID参数区域与最坏情况线性模型导出的必要条件边界之间存在分离，保守性较大，需要更紧的充分条件来缩小这一差距。

Method: 提出端点平衡的二次加积分Lyapunov证书（Endpoint-Balanced Quadratic-Plus-Integral），关键在于选择二次交叉项系数，使得混合项惩罚在可容许有效阻尼区间的两个端点处达到平衡，再提取标量PID不等式。

Result: 当速度导数上界为正时，所提条件认证的固定增益PID区域严格包含Zhao-Guo和Zhang-Guo方法认证的区域；当该上界为零时，认证边界与必要边界重合。该方法在Lyapunov分析层面降低了混合项惩罚在整个有效阻尼区间上的均匀性。

Conclusion: 所提出的端点平衡Lyapunov构造有效降低了固定增益PID控制器针对二阶非线性不确定系统的鲁棒镇定分析的保守性，提供了更紧的充分条件。

Abstract: This letter studies robust set-point regulation of scalar second-order nonlinear uncertain systems using a classical PID controller with constant gains. The scalar second-order model provides a minimal prototype for nonlinear mechanical and electromechanical dynamics, while its velocity-dependent term captures uncertainties such as physical damping and friction. For a positive velocity-derivative bound, existing Lyapunov sufficient conditions certify fixed-gain PID parameter regions that remain separated from the boundary associated with the necessary condition obtained from the worst-case linear model. To reduce this conservatism, this letter proposes an endpoint-balanced quadratic-plus-integral Lyapunov certificate. The key idea is to choose the quadratic cross-term coefficient so that the mixed-term penalty is balanced at the two endpoints of the admissible effective-damping interval before extracting the scalar PID inequality. The resulting condition guarantees global asymptotic regulation for the full derivative-bounded uncertainty class. When the velocity-derivative bound is positive, the proposed condition certifies a fixed-gain PID region that strictly contains those certified by Zhao--Guo and Zhang--Guo. When this bound is zero, the corresponding boundary coincides with that necessary boundary. At the level of Lyapunov analysis, the construction reduces the uniform mixed-term penalty over the entire effective-damping interval.

</details>


### [237] [Class-Based Smart Charging Control for Electric Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03319)
*Giuseppe C. Calafiore,Luca Ambrosino,Matteo Della Rossa,Laurent El Ghaoui*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于随机控制框架的电动汽车充电站运营策略，通过将车辆按剩余充电需求分类聚合，联合优化充电动作与能量管理，在保证可扩展性的同时实现充电成本与服务质量间的帕累托权衡，仿真显示最多可降低17.5%的单千瓦时成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向配备光伏发电和电池储能的大型电动汽车充电站，现有方法难以兼顾大规模车队下的可扩展性、随机性处理以及经济与服务质量的平衡，亟需一种既能保持状态简洁又能联合优化充电与能量管理的控制框架。

Method: 将车辆按剩余充电需求聚合为有限类，建立离散时间随机动态模型（含随机到达、类间转移、随机离开、光伏发电、电池储能及电网交互）；基于期望模型构建有限时域智能充电问题，联合优化类级充电动作与能量管理变量，阶段问题为线性规划；采用收缩时域在线求解，首动作经整数离散化后实施；进一步推导鲁棒对偶形式以应对到达、离开和光伏一阶矩的区间不确定性。

Result: 在9种配置（3种电价模式×3种到达曲线）下进行仿真，相比服务贪婪的FIFO基线，所提控制器在服务水平略低的情况下，单千瓦时成本降低最多17.5%，日均成本降幅更大，验证了可扩展性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提控制器在大型充电站中实现了经济效率与充电服务质量之间可调谐的帕累托权衡，证明了聚合分类策略与鲁棒优化相结合在实际运营中的有效性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a stochastic control framework for the operation of electric-vehicle (EV) charging stations equipped with on-site photovoltaic (PV) generation and battery storage. To preserve scalability for large fleets, vehicles are aggregated into a finite number of classes according to their residual charging demand, yielding a compact state description and avoiding vehicle-level optimization. The charging-station dynamics are modeled in discrete time and capture stochastic arrivals, charging-induced class transitions, stochastic departures, PV generation, battery operation, and power exchange with the grid. Based on the corresponding expectation model, we formulate a finite-horizon smart-charging problem that jointly optimizes class-wise charging actions and energy-management variables to balance electricity-purchase cost and transitions toward lower residual-demand classes. The resulting stage problem is a linear program, solved in shrinking-horizon form and implemented online after integer discretization of the first charging action. We also derive a robust counterpart that preserves feasibility under interval uncertainty on the first moments of arrivals, departures, and PV generation. To validate scalability and robustness, we conduct an extensive simulation campaign across nine configurations, combining three real-world electricity price patterns with three EV-arrival profiles. Numerical results show that, at comparable but slightly lower service levels, the proposed controller reduces the cost per kWh by up to 17.5% relative to a service-greedy First-In-First-Out baseline; the total daily cost decreases even more substantially because the controller avoids economically unattractive charging. Overall, the results demonstrate a tunable Pareto trade-off between economic efficiency and charging-service quality for large-scale charging hubs.

</details>


### [238] [A Recursive Domain- and Objective-Adaptive Frank-Wolfe Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03383)
*Marcel Kaiser,Tobias Sutter*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种递归变体的Frank-Wolfe算法，用于在目标和约束域均未知的数据驱动场景下进行凸优化，该算法通过集成估计器保持无投影特性，并建立了收敛保证，实验表明其性能接近经典Frank-Wolfe算法且计算成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Frank-Wolfe算法假设问题域和目标函数完全已知，但在许多实际数据驱动场景中，这些信息最初未知且必须从数据中学习。现有方法无法有效处理这种双重不确定性。

Method: 提出递归Frank-Wolfe变体，将估计器集成到优化过程中，迭代地改进对问题域和目标函数的近似，同时保留经典算法无投影的特性。

Result: 建立了收敛保证，表明优化误差随所学习估计器的精度缩放。两个实验验证了该方法在达到与经典Frank-Wolfe算法相当收敛行为的同时，提供了显著的计算节省。

Conclusion: 所提出的递归Frank-Wolfe方法能有效处理数据驱动场景下的优化问题，在保持无投影优势的同时实现可证明的收敛性能，适用于问题域和目标函数均需从数据中学习的实际应用。

Abstract: We investigate a recursive variant of the classical Frank-Wolfe algorithm for minimizing a convex differentiable function over a convex compact domain. Unlike the traditional setting, we assume that both the problem domain and the objective function are initially unknown and must be learned from data. To address this, we integrate estimators into the optimization process, allowing the algorithm to iteratively refine approximations of the problem domain and the objective function. Our approach maintains the projection-free nature of the classical Frank-Wolfe algorithm while adapting to the uncertainty inherent in data-driven settings. We establish convergence guarantees for the recursive method, showing that the optimization error scales with the accuracy of the learned estimators. Two experiments support our theoretical findings, demonstrating that the proposed method achieves convergence behavior comparable to that of the classical Frank-Wolfe algorithm under exact knowledge of the problem domain and objective function, while offering significant computational savings.

</details>


### [239] [Optimizing Explicit Unit-Distance Lower-Bound Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03419)
*Michael T. M. Emmerich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对Erdős单位距离猜想被证伪后的参数优化问题，将Sawin显式定量改进中的有限参数选择建模为非线性整数规划，提出轻量级开源Python验证流程。通过贪心构造、定制整数进化策略及离散重组方法，得到四个优化证书，最佳结果支持$u(n)>n^{1.0152}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős单位距离猜想已被证伪，Sawin给出显式定量改进但其中有限参数（素数集T和S_Q、整数重数k(p)、有理参数R）的选择未充分优化，需系统性计算方法以提升下界。

Method: 将参数选择问题建模为非线性整数规划；设计确定性贪心构造启发式、带修复算子的定制整数进化策略、双亲离散重组变异；构建Python验证流水线，先复现Sawin原参数再优化。

Result: 获得四个优化证书：Sawin原版δ=0.0141144…；贪心优化δ=0.0151718…；定制进化策略（R=6672416/100000，δ=0.01526166…）；定制进化策略+离散重组（相同R，δ=0.01526286…）。最佳证书支持$u(n)>n^{1.0152}$。

Conclusion: 在Sawin显式准则严格应用前提下，最优证书可将单位距离下界提升至$n^{1.0152}$，且方法轻量可复现。

Abstract: The 2026 disproof of Erdős's unit-distance conjecture and Sawin's subsequent explicit quantitative refinement show that the maximum number $u(n)$ of unit distances among $n$ planar points can exceed $n^{1+\varepsilon}$ for a fixed positive $\varepsilon$. Sawin's explicit bound gives more than $n^{1.014}$ unit distances for arbitrarily large $n$ and exposes finite parameters whose choice is not fully optimized. This report formulates the finite parameter-selection task as a variant of a nonlinear integer programming problem and proposes an open-source Python verification pipeline, first validated by reproducing Sawin's published parameter choice and then applied to computationally improved certificates. The main computational contribution is an integer optimization and checking procedure for the sets of primes $T$ and $S_Q$, the integer multiplicities $k(p)$, and a rationally encoded real parameter $R$. The optimization pipelines are intentionally lightweight and replicable on standard hardware: we propose a deterministic greedy construction heuristic, a Tailored Integer Evolution Strategy with repair operators for number-theoretic feasibility, and a two-parent discrete-recombination variant. Four certificate levels are compared: Sawin's published example with $δ=0.0141144286784982\ldots$, a greedy optimization certificate with $δ=0.0151718056372133\ldots$, a Tailored Integer Evolution Strategy certificate with rational $R=6672416/100000$ and $δ=0.0152616610684193\ldots$, and a Tailored Integer Evolution Strategy with discrete recombination, again with $R=6672416/100000$, giving $δ=0.0152628688170072\ldots$. Consequently, subject to Sawin's explicit criterion being applied exactly as cited, the best current certificate supports the cautious clean statement $u(n)>n^{1.0152}$ for arbitrarily large $n$.

</details>


### [240] [Optimal Finite-Horizon LQR Control for Traffic Flow via Variable Speed Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03632)
*Brian Block,Stephanie Stockar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对具有三角形基本图的一阶Lighthill-Whitham-Richards交通模型，提出了有限时域线性二次型调节器，通过可变限速实现域内控制，并利用参数特征线法解析求解Riccati偏微分方程，相比无限时域LQR显著提升了控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多采用无限时域LQR框架，但在时敏交通管理场景中，有限时域问题能更好地满足实际需求。本文旨在填补有限时域LQR在双曲型偏微分方程交通模型上的空白。

Method: 建立了有限时域LQR框架，将可变限速作为源项纳入控制，推导出时空变化的状态反馈函数；通过求解Riccati PDE获得最优控制律，并采用参数特征线法解析求解该非线性PDE。

Result: Riccati PDE的解是时间和空间以及交通状态的函数；敏感性分析展示了两种时域下LQR参数在不同交通场景中的影响；仿真验证了有限时域LQR能保证有限时间收敛，且控制性能显著优于无限时域LQR。

Conclusion: 所提出的有限时域LQR方法特别适用于时敏交通管理应用，在多种场景下均实现了更优的控制效果。

Abstract: This article presents a finite-horizon linear quadratic regulator for the control of the first-order Lighthill-Whitham-Richards traffic model with a triangular fundamental diagram. The in-domain control action is realized through variable speed limits implemented as a source term in the governing hyperbolic partial differential equation. Unlike prior studies on infinite-horizon formulations, this article develops a finite-horizon LQR framework, deriving a space and time varying state feedback function for hyperbolic PDEs. The solution to the finite time optimal control problem relies on the solution of another PDE, called the Riccati PDE. The resulting nonlinear Riccati PDE is solved analytically via the parametric method of characteristics. The Riccati PDE solution is a function of both time and space, as well as the traffic regime. A sensitivity analysis demonstrates the effects of the LQR parameters for both the infinite and finite time horizon problem in different traffic situations, while siulations validate the finite-horizon LQR's ability to guarentee finite-time convergence. Comapred to the infinite-horizon LQR, the proposed approach achieves significantly improved control performance across various scenarios, making it particularly suitable for time-sensitive traffic management applications.

</details>


### [241] [Bregman meets Lévy: Stochastic mirror descent with heavy-tailed noise in continuous and discrete time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03769)
*Pierre-Louis Cauvin,Panayotis Mertikopoulos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了重尾噪声下随机镜像下降(SMD)的鲁棒性，通过引入由 Lévy 噪声驱动的连续时间模型(Lévy mirror flow, LMF)，证明了即使在无限方差情况下，LMF 在凸和强凸目标下仍能达到 ε-最优，并给出了相应的收敛时间界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究 SMD 在重尾噪声（随机梯度具有无限方差）下是否仍能保持其收敛保证，以严谨方式回答该基础性问题。

Method: 引入由中心化 Lévy 噪声过程驱动的随机微分方程作为 SMD 的连续时间模型（Lévy mirror flow, LMF），该模型是重尾噪声下 SMD 的尺度极限。

Result: 在凸情形下，LMF 在 $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-p/(p-1)})$ 时间内达到 ε-最优；在（相对）强凸目标下，在 $\mathcal{\tilde O}(\varepsilon^{-1/(p-1)})$ 时间内达到 ε-最优。这些结果刻画了频繁长跳跃对收敛过程的影响，并推广到重尾噪声下多种 SMD 变体的离散时间保证。

Conclusion: 尽管 LMF 轨迹存在任意幅度的跳跃不连续性且可能导致无限方差，但该方法仍能收敛，且收敛速率与噪声重尾程度 $p$ 明确相关。

Abstract: We study the robustness of stochastic mirror descent (SMD) under heavy-tailed noise, focusing on whether the method retains its convergence guarantees when run with infinite-variance stochastic gradient input. To address this question in a principled manner, we begin by introducing a continuous-time model of SMD as a stochastic differential equation (SDE) driven by a centered Lévy noise process with finite $p$-th order moments, $1 < p \leq 2$. This scheme -- which we call the Lévy mirror flow (LMF) -- arises naturally as the scaling limit of SMD in the presence of heavy-tailed noise. In particular, when $p < 2$ -- the heavy noise regime -- the trajectories of LMF generically exhibit jump discontinuities of arbitrary magnitude which, if frequent enough, lead to infinite variance. Nonetheless, despite this highly singular behavior, we show that LMF attains $ε$-optimality within $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-p/(p-1)})$ time in the convex case, and within $\mathcal{\tilde O}(ε^{-1/(p-1)})$ time for (relatively) strongly convex objectives. These guarantees provide a transparent characterization of the impact of frequent long jumps on the convergence of the process, and percolate to a series of matching discrete-time guarantees for several variants of SMD under heavy-tailed noise.

</details>


### [242] [Introduction to stochastic gradient methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03953)
*Simon Weissmann*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 这些讲义介绍了基于梯度的确定性优化方法及其收敛性理论，并重点阐述了随机梯度方法在机器学习中的收敛性分析，包括几乎必然收敛、期望收敛、经典收敛率以及方差缩减等高级主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为一阶优化方法（特别是随机梯度方法）提供系统性的理论介绍，满足机器学习和统计学中对大规模优化算法收敛性分析的需求。

Method: 从确定性梯度方法出发，在光滑性、凸性、强凸性和Polyak-Lojasiewicz条件等标准假设下分析收敛性；进而转向随机逼近和随机梯度下降，研究几乎必然收敛和期望收敛，推导经典收敛率，并讨论几乎必然收敛率和方差缩减方法等高级主题。

Result: 建立了确定性梯度方法和随机梯度方法在不同假设条件下的收敛性理论框架，给出了相应的收敛率结果。

Conclusion: 该讲义系统性地梳理了一阶优化方法的收敛理论，为理解和分析随机梯度方法提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: These lecture notes provide an introduction to first-order optimization methods with a particular emphasis on stochastic gradient methods. We begin with deterministic gradient based methods for unconstrained optimization and study their convergence under standard assumptions such as smoothness, convexity, strong convexity, and the Polyak-Lojasiewicz condition. We then turn to stochastic approximation and stochastic gradient descent, motivated by empirical and expected risk minimization in machine learning. The main focus is on convergence theory: we discuss almost sure convergence and convergence in expectation, derive classical convergence rates, and present selected advanced topics, including almost sure convergence rates and variance reduction methods.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [243] [Position: Prioritize Identifying Structure, Not Complex Models, for Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02632)
*Tyler H. McCormick*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文论证了现代机器学习（尤其是大语言模型）在高维代理场景下进行机制学习时存在普遍的不确定性：多种不相容的机制可能在观测数据上产生相同的关系，因此预测成功和连贯解释不足以证明机制发现。LLM会进一步将大量等价解释压缩成单一流畅叙述，加剧这一问题。论文提出了"机制ML"的具体标准，以确保LLM工作流真正支持科学而非仅仅是模拟科学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示现代ML/AI模型（特别是LLM）在从观测数据生成科学假设和机制解释时存在的根本性认识论问题——机制学习的欠定性问题，并警示这一问题在LLM中被放大。

Method: 通过理论论证和概念分析，指出在高维代理区间中多种机制与观测数据等价的现象，并分析LLM因追求叙述流畅性而掩盖这种不确定性的特殊风险。

Result: 论证了预测成功和解释连贯性不足以作为机制发现的证据；指出了LLM倾向于将大类的等价解释坍缩为单一流畅叙述的特殊危害。

Conclusion: 提出了"机制ML"的具体规范标准，强调这些标准对于确保LLM工作流真正服务于科学研究而非模拟科学是必要的。

Abstract: Modern Machine Learning (ML) and Artificial Intelligence (AI) models, especially large language models (LLMs), are increasingly used to generate scientific hypotheses and mechanistic explanations from observational data. This position paper argues that in the high-dimensional proxy regimes where modern ML excels, mechanistic learning is generically underdetermined: many incompatible mechanisms induce essentially the same observational relationships on the support of the data, so predictive success and coherent explanations are insufficient evidence of mechanism discovery. This underdetermination becomes uniquely hazardous with large language models (LLMs), which tend to collapse large equivalence classes of explanations into a single fluent narrative. This paper proposes concrete standards for ``mechanistic ML,'' and argues these norms are necessary if LLM-centered workflows are to support science rather than merely simulate it.

</details>


### [244] [Target Updates May Stabilize Linear Q-Learning: Periodic and Soft Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02645)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对线性Q学习中的周期性硬目标更新和软目标更新机制进行了严格的理论分析，证明了在特定谱条件和步长条件下，这两种机制能保证收敛到精确的投影Q-Bellman解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Q学习和actor-critic方法中的目标更新机制（周期性硬更新和软更新）是经验上有效的稳定技术，但其精确的理论解释尚不完整，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 利用Bellman最大值诱导的精确切换线性系统（SLS）动力学以及切换矩阵族的联合谱半径（JSR），对线性函数逼近的Q学习（线性Q学习）进行严格分析。先分析确定性线性Q学习中的目标更新机制，再通过用随机采样模式替代确定性模式并加入随机噪声分析，推广到随机强化学习设置。

Result: 证明了在明确的谱条件和步长条件下，周期性硬目标更新和软目标更新可以保证线性Q学习收敛到精确的投影Q-Bellman解。

Conclusion: 为目标更新机制在强化学习中的稳定性提供了严格的理论基础，建立了从确定性子到随机环境的完整分析框架。

Abstract: Periodic target updates in Q-learning and soft target updates in actor-critic methods are empirically well established stabilization mechanisms, but their precise theoretical explanation is still incomplete. This paper gives a rigorous and exact analysis of these mechanisms for Q-learning with linear function approximation (linear Q-learning) using the exact switched linear system (SLS) dynamics induced by the Bellman maximum and the joint spectral radius (JSR) of the resulting switching matrix families. Although linear Q-learning can fail to converge in general, we prove that, under explicit spectral and step-size conditions, periodic hard target updates and soft target updates can guarantee convergence to the exact projected Q-Bellman solution. The main analysis is carried out for deterministic linear Q-learning, where the target-update mechanism is most transparent. Once the corresponding JSR certificate is established for the mean recursion, the stochastic reinforcement-learning setting can be treated by replacing deterministic modes with sampled stochastic modes and adding the corresponding stochastic-noise analysis.

</details>


### [245] [State-Coupled Volatility in Latent Dynamical Systems: Recovery Under Partial Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02664)
*Imani Beckett*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种状态耦合随机波动率框架，用于在潜在状态空间模型中估计依赖于潜在状态位置的变异性，并通过粒子期望最大化方法在部分观测条件下实现参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在状态空间模型通常假设过程变异性与潜在状态位置无关，但在生物学、行为学等系统中，变异性可能依赖于潜在动态状态，现有恒定方差模型无法捕捉这种结构化随机性。

Method: 提出状态耦合随机波动率框架，使潜在过程方差依赖于与潜在平衡点的位移；开发结合自举粒子滤波和后向轨迹平滑的粒子期望最大化（EM）程序进行参数估计；引入耦合参数γ量化状态位置与过程变异性的关联强度。

Result: 大规模仿真基准测试表明：所提框架相比观测状态异方差代理方法持续降低了恢复偏差，在强耦合下改进最大；恢复性能随潜在持续性增加而改善；检测性能在广泛条件下具有竞争力，且随观测噪声增加而更具优势。

Conclusion: 状态耦合波动率可以在部分观测条件下被识别和估计，该框架为研究状态依赖变异性提供了方法论基础，有助于评估结构化随机性是否提供了超越均值状态轨迹的额外动力学信息。

Abstract: Latent state-space models are widely used to study partially observed dynamical systems, yet most formulations assume that process variability is independent of latent-state position. In many biological, behavioral, and physiological systems, however, variability may depend systematically on the underlying dynamical state, producing structured stochasticity that is not captured by constant-variance models. We introduce a state-coupled stochastic volatility framework in which latent process variance depends on displacement from a latent equilibrium. To estimate this relationship under partial observation, we develop a particle expectation-maximization procedure combining bootstrap particle filtering and backward trajectory smoothing. The model includes a coupling parameter, $γ$, that quantifies the strength of association between latent-state position and process variability. A large-scale simulation benchmark evaluated recovery and detection performance across varying coupling strengths, observation noise levels, trajectory lengths, and persistence regimes. The proposed framework consistently reduced recovery bias relative to an observed-state heteroskedastic proxy, with the largest improvements occurring under strong coupling. Recovery performance improved with increasing latent persistence, while detection performance remained competitive across a broad range of conditions and became increasingly advantageous as observation noise increased. Taken together, the results demonstrate that state-coupled volatility can be identified and estimated under partial observation when latent-state structure is explicitly modeled. The framework provides a practical methodological foundation for studying state-dependent variability and evaluating whether structured stochasticity contributes information about system dynamics beyond that contained in mean-state trajectories alone.

</details>


### [246] [ScoreStop: Gradient-based early stopping using functional score tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02740)
*Oliver J. Hines,Christian L. Hines*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出ScoreStop，一种基于梯度检验的早停规则，将每轮迭代的停止决策转化为检验当前预测器是否为总体风险最小化者的假设检验，使用函数化得分检验统计量，具有尺度不变性和已知渐近分布，适用于显式、隐式及数据依赖损失函数，实验表明其与基于损失的方法性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度提升决策树的早停规则依赖验证集损失和固定的耐心参数，但该参数缺乏可解释尺度，且验证损失可能含噪声或由用户自定义梯度隐式定义（如LambdaRank）或数据依赖（如Cox回归），需要一种更具统计原理的早停方法。

Method: ScoreStop：在每次迭代中，将停止决策形式化为检验当前预测器是否为总体风险最小化者的原假设，基于验证数据计算函数化得分检验统计量，该统计量在更新方向上具有尺度不变性，且在原假设下具有已知渐近分布。

Result: 在合成实验和真实数据基准测试中，ScoreStop与基于损失的方法性能相当。

Conclusion: 基于梯度的检验方法为早停提供了一种统计上有原则的替代方案，且天然适用于传统损失方法难以处理的隐式损失和数据依赖损失场景。

Abstract: Gradient boosted decision trees require a stopping rule to avoid overfitting. The standard rule monitors a validation loss and stops if the loss fails to improve for a fixed patience period. However, the patience parameter has no interpretable scale and validation losses can be noisy or implicitly defined by a user-specified gradient. We propose ScoreStop, a gradient-based early-stopping rule that casts the stopping decision at each iteration as a test of the null hypothesis that the current predictor is the population risk minimizer. We use a functional score test, computed on validation data, with a statistic that is scale-invariant in the update direction, with a known asymptotic distribution under the null. Because our test uses gradients rather than loss values, the same construction applies to implicit losses such as LambdaRank, and data-dependent losses such as Cox regression via influence functions. In synthetic experiments and real-data benchmarks, we show that ScoreStop is competitive with loss-based methods.

</details>


### [247] [Scalable Derivative Gaussian Processes via Exact Gradient Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02909)
*Hyunseok Seung,Matthias Katzfuss*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: TERA提出了一种基于目标特定精确梯度缩减的高度可扩展导数高斯过程方法，通过证明平稳核下梯度分量与目标函数值的条件独立性，利用Vecchia近似将计算复杂度从O(n³d³)降至O(dm²+m⁶)，实现与维度d近似无关的高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维场景中，梯度观测可显著提升高斯过程代理模型的精度，但标准导数GP的精确推理需处理n个函数值和n个全梯度，计算复杂度高达O(n³d³)，难以扩展到高维大样本情形。

Method: 1. 理论证明：对于平稳核，目标点与条件点连线的正交梯度分量条件独立于目标函数值，因此条件密度至多由m²个方向导数完全刻画。2. 基于此缩减条件，将其作为Vecchia近似的局部因子，实现n与d从稠密矩阵求逆中解耦，每目标评估成本降至O(dm²+m⁶)时间和O(dm²+m⁴)内存。

Result: TERA在保持底层导数GP模型数学不变的前提下，实现了最优的预测精度，同时计算速度比标准导数GP快数个数量级，且计算时间和GPU峰值内存随d增长保持基本平稳。

Conclusion: TERA通过理论驱动的梯度缩减策略，有效解决了导数GP在高维场景下的计算瓶颈，实现了高度可扩展的高维推理。

Abstract: Gradient observations can substantially improve Gaussian process (GP) surrogates, particularly in high-dimensional settings where function evaluations are expensive. However, exact inference with $n$ function values and $n$ full gradients in $d$ dimensions scales cubically in the joint state size, imposing an intractable $\mathcal{O}(n^3 d^3)$ computational bottleneck. We introduce TERA, a highly scalable derivative GP method based on target-specific exact gradient reduction. We prove that for stationary kernels, the gradient components orthogonal to the directions connecting the target and conditioning points are conditionally independent of the target function value; consequently, the exact conditional density is fully characterized by at most $m^2$ directional derivatives once a conditioning set of size $m$ is specified. By using these reduced, dimension-free conditionals as local factors in a Vecchia approximation, TERA effectively decouples $n$ and $d$ from the dense matrix inversion. This reduces the per-target evaluation cost to $\mathcal{O}(dm^2 + m^6)$ time and $\mathcal{O}(dm^2 + m^4)$ memory, leaving the underlying derivative GP model mathematically unchanged. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that TERA achieves state-of-the-art predictive accuracy while operating orders of magnitude faster than standard derivative GPs. Crucially, both computation time and peak GPU memory remain essentially flat with respect to $d$, enabling highly scalable inference in high-dimensional spaces.

</details>


### [248] [Trajectory-Aware Node Contributions and the Limits of Static Controllability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03067)
*Valentina Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Michael Coppedge*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出“涌现贡献度（EC）”，一种基于轨迹的节点动态杠杆度量方法，可在非线性时变系统中评估节点对系统行为的贡献，并系统刻画了其与线性时不变系统中平均可控性指标的一致与分歧条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有节点贡献度量方法（静态图中心性或可控性Gramian）假设线性时不变动力学，无法处理真实系统中普遍存在的非线性与时变特性，亟需一种适用于更广泛动力系统的节点贡献度量框架。

Method: 定义有限时间窗口内的涌现贡献度EC，基于系统轨迹上的脉冲响应能量（Jacobian矩阵计算），与模型无关且在线性时不变极限下退化为平均可控性；通过构造已知真实贡献的合成系统族，扫描非线性度、动态结构、持久性和扰动幅度四个维度，构建相图比较EC与平均可控性的一致/分歧区域；在五个真实系统上验证相图诊断价值。

Result: EC与平均可控性在静态或平滑漂移动态下一致，在持久性符号反转的动态切换下分歧最大；极端扰动幅度下两者均失效，标定局部线性化的极限。在真实系统中发现方差-杠杆解离现象：部分节点扰动传播广泛但系统内方差低，该模式不被静态中心性或方差摘要捕获。

Conclusion: EC为非线性时变系统中节点动态贡献提供更精细的刻画，其与平均可控性的分歧程度可作为判断是否值得投入额外计算成本的诊断依据；方差-杠杆解离揭示了EC在发现隐藏关键节点方面的独特价值。

Abstract: A recurring data mining task in complex networks is to determine how individual nodes contribute to system behavior. Existing approaches rely on either static-graph centralities or control-theoretic quantities such as controllability Gramians, which assume linear, time-invariant dynamics. Estimated systems, however, are typically nonlinear and time-varying. We define "emergent contribution (EC)," a finite-horizon measure of a node's dynamical leverage: the metric-weighted energy of its impulse response accumulated along the system trajectory. Computed from the Jacobians of any differentiable model, EC is estimator-agnostic and reduces exactly to average controllability in the linear, time-invariant limit. Our contribution is a characterization of when the two measures agree and diverge. Using a controlled synthetic family with known ground-truth contribution, we construct a phase diagram spanning nonlinearity, regime structure, persistence, and perturbation amplitude. EC and average controllability agree under static or smoothly drifting dynamics and both track ground truth. Divergence emerges under persistent regime switching, is strongest under persistent sign reversal, and disappears when the sign reversal is removed. At extreme perturbation amplitudes, both measures degrade, identifying the limits of local linearization. We place five estimated real systems from several domains within this phase space. Their placement serves as a diagnostic of when EC provides information beyond static controllability and therefore justifies its additional computational cost. On one panel examined in depth, a twenty-seed retraining ensemble reveals a robust variance--leverage dissociation: nodes whose perturbations propagate widely despite low within-system variance, which is not recovered by static centralities nor variance-based summaries.

</details>


### [249] [An Asymptotic Theory of Chain-of-Thought in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03217)
*Kaito Takanami,Cengiz Pehlevan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过线性回归的上下文权重预测这一可解模型，理论分析了链式思维推理深度对泛化误差的影响，揭示了指数/多项式改进、饱和与过度思考之间的相变现象，并指出最优推理深度依赖于预训练数据量和上下文长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维推理已广泛应用于大语言模型，但其推理深度与泛化能力之间的缩放规律尚不明确，缺乏理论层面的理解。

Method: 构建线性回归中上下文权重预测的理论可解模型，将测试时推理建模为权重参数估计的迭代精炼过程，利用随机矩阵理论在高维渐进极限下推导出泛化误差关于推理深度、预训练数据量和上下文长度的精确表达式。

Result: 发现了从指数改进到多项式改进、饱和以及过度思考之间的尖锐相变；刻画了最优推理深度的缩放规律；证明了深层推理仅在预训练和上下文信息足够丰富时才有效，否则会导致误差放大或饱和。

Conclusion: 为测试时链式思维推理深度如何影响泛化性能提供了统一的理论解释，并通过线性注意力和softmax注意力模型的实验验证了理论预测。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has become a widely used mechanism for eliciting multi-step reasoning in large language models by generating intermediate reasoning steps at inference time. Yet the scaling behavior of generalization with CoT depth remains poorly understood. To address this question, we study a theoretically solvable model of CoT for in-context weight prediction in linear regression, where test-time reasoning is represented as an iterative refinement of the weight-parameter estimate. Using tools from random matrix theory under high-dimensional asymptotics, we derive an exact formula for the generalization error as a function of reasoning depth, pretraining data amount, and context length. Our analysis reveals a sharp phase transition separating exponential and polynomial improvement, saturation, and overthinking, and characterizes how the optimal reasoning depth scales. We further show that deeper reasoning is most effective with sufficiently rich pretraining and in-context information, whereas limited pretraining or context makes longer reasoning prone to error amplification or saturation. We also validate these predictions through experiments on fully learned linear attention and softmax attention models. Our results provide a unified theoretical account of how test-time CoT depth affects generalization.

</details>


### [250] [Hierarchies of Calibration: Classification meets Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03245)
*Johannes Resin,Lu Yang,Tilmann Gneiting*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文综述、扩展并衔接了分类与回归任务中提出的多种校准概念，重点关注不同概念之间的层次关系，涵盖连续、计数、名义分类及二元结果等数据类型。主要贡献包括：引入名义结果的模态校准概念，区分完全、部分和平均校准，证明双概率积分变换（PIT）校准与离散结果已有校准概念逻辑独立，并推广了基于预测分布性质（如均值、分位数、事件概率）的校准结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率预测与观测结果之间的兼容性（即校准）是评估预测系统质量的核心。然而，针对分类和回归任务的多种校准概念分散于文献中，缺乏系统的梳理与层次化整合。本文旨在统一这些概念，厘清其逻辑关系，并填补名义结果和离散结果校准理论中的空白。

Method: 采用理论分析与形式化定义相结合的方法，系统回顾了现有校准概念，引入新的校准类别（如模态校准），通过逻辑关系图构建层次化分类体系，并借助反例与工作实例验证概念间的包含、等价或独立关系。

Result: （1）提出了名义结果的模态校准，并区分了完全、部分和平均校准三个层次；（2）证明了双PIT校准与离散结果中已有的校准概念逻辑上相互独立；（3）将基于预测分布泛函（均值、分位数、事件概率）的校准结果推广到更一般的数据类型；（4）提供了用于构建教学性示例和反例的算法工具。

Conclusion: 本文为校准理论提供了统一的框架，明确了不同校准概念在各类数据场景下的层次关系，指出了名义结果和离散结果校准中的新发现，有助于指导概率预测系统的设计与评估。

Abstract: Concepts of calibration formalize the compatibility between probabilistic predictions and the respective outcomes. In a nutshell, the outcomes ought to be indistinguishable from random draws from the predictive distributions. In this paper, we review, extend, and bridge notions of calibration that have been proposed for classification and regression tasks. Particular emphasis is given to hierarchical relations between the various notions, as they apply to general real-valued data, continuous outcomes, count data, nominal classes, and binary outcomes. To highlight a number of contributions, we introduce the notion of modal calibration for nominal outcomes, we distinguish full, partial, and average calibration in this setting, and we show that double probability integral transform (PIT) calibration is logically independent of previously proposed concepts of calibration for discrete outcomes. Furthermore, we generalize extant results on concepts of calibration that are expressed in terms of properties or functionals of the predictive distributions, such as means, quantiles, or event probabilities. Throughout the paper, we illustrate the concepts and their hierarchical relations in worked examples, and we provide algorithmic tools that support the construction of instructive examples and counterexamples.

</details>


### [251] [Combining Statistical Features and Deep Encodings for Rehearsal-Based Class-Incremental Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03292)
*Pablo García-Santaclara,Bruno Fernández-Castro,Rebeca Pilar Díaz-Redondo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双流特征提取管道（结合预训练冻结基础模型的深度时序嵌入特征和统计特征）的方法，用于多变量时间序列的类增量持续学习，在五个基准数据集上实现了有竞争力的平均准确率和低遗忘率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实环境中的系统需要在不遗忘先前所学知识的情况下添加新类别并整合新信息，而多变量时间序列数据的时序结构使得这一任务更加复杂。

Method: 构建双流特征提取管道：一条流使用预训练冻结的基础模型生成深度时序嵌入特征，另一条流应用统计特征；结合两者进行类增量持续学习分类。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上，所提系统在所有数据集上均达到有竞争力的平均准确率，并在所有实验配置下保持低遗忘率。

Conclusion: 双流特征提取方法有效解决了多变量时间序列类增量持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，兼顾了深度特征与统计特征的优势。

Abstract: Many systems used in real-world environments require adding new categories and incorporating new information without forgetting what was previously learnt by the classification model. This is known as class-incremental continual learning, and in the case of multivariate time-series, is further complicated by the temporal structure of the data. In this paper, we present a novel approach for performing class incremental continual learning for the classification of multivariate time series data based upon the construction of a dual-stream feature extraction pipeline (using both deep temporal embedding features generated via a pre-trained frozen foundation model and application of statistical features). Evaluated on five benchmark datasets, the proposed system achieves competitive average accuracy across all datasets while maintaining low forgetting rates across all experimental configurations.

</details>


### [252] [A Robust Optimization Approach to Sparse Principal Component Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03553)
*David Vävinggren,Francis Bach,André M. H. Teixeira,Dave Zachariah,Antônio H. Ribeiro*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: AdvPCA通过对抗性鲁棒优化实现稀疏PCA，无需显式L1正则化调参，并提出数据自适应的无参数化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PCA产生稠密表示，不适合高维数据；现有稀疏PCA方法依赖L1惩罚项，但无监督任务中惩罚系数难以调节。

Method: 将稀疏性目标转化为对抗性鲁棒优化问题，对潜空间施加有界最坏情况扰动；推导出闭式解，提出迭代算法：稀疏编码器通过对抗性线性回归更新，解码器通过正交更新。

Result: 理论刻画了最优解结构，导出了数据自适应的参数化方案，使算法无需手动调参即可有效运行；合成数据和真实基因组数据实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: AdvPCA提供了一种无需调参的稀疏PCA新范式，在保持降维效果的同时实现了自然稀疏性。

Abstract: While principal component analysis (PCA) is a fundamental tool for dimensionality reduction, its dense representations make it ill-suited for high-dimensional data. Existing methods address this by promoting sparsity through explicit $\ell_1$-penalties, but these are not obvious to tune due to the unsupervised nature of the task. In contrast, we propose Adversarial PCA (AdvPCA), which leverages robust optimization to achieve sparsity by optimizing the reconstruction objective against bounded, worst-case latent space perturbations. We show that this formulation admits a closed-form reduction, leading to a practical iterative algorithm that alternates between adversarial linear regression-style updates for the sparse encoder and orthogonal updates for the decoder. By theoretically characterizing the solution, we derive a data-adaptive parameterization that allows the algorithm to perform effectively out of the box. We validate these claims through numerical experiments on synthetic and real-world genomics data.

</details>


### [253] [Few-Shot Prediction for Pulsar Noise with Long Short-Term Memory Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03574)
*Qingye Tang,Dechao An,Haoran Peng,Yuqi Ouyang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型无关元学习（MAML）优化的LSTM网络，结合粒子群优化算法进行超参数自动调优，仅需10%的时序残差数据即可在脉冲星计时阵列高频域实现高精度预测，模型仅占用16.86 MB内存，单步预测仅需18毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决脉冲星计时残差预测中数据稀缺的关键挑战，特别是在脉冲星计时阵列数据集的不同自旋频率子组中数据量有限的问题。

Method: 采用LSTM网络结合模型无关元学习（MAML）算法实现快速域适应，通过少量真实计时残差进行微调；使用粒子群优化算法自动优化超参数，提升预测精度。

Result: 在国际脉冲星计时阵列（IPTA）第二数据发布上，仅需高频测试域10%的时序残差进行微调，即在三个评估指标上均实现了准确预测；模型仅消耗16.86 MB CPU内存和18毫秒单步预测时间。

Conclusion: 该轻量级解决方案在资源受限环境（计算能力、内存或能量有限）中具有高度适用性，能够实现高效实时的脉冲星计时残差预测。

Abstract: This work proposes a novel solution to predict pulsar timing residuals with limited data, addressing the critical challenge of data scarcity across spin-frequency subgroups of millisecond pulsars in PTA datasets. The proposed solution applies a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) network optimized using the model-agnostic meta-learning algorithm, enabling rapid adaptation to new frequency domain by fine-tuning the LSTM network with only a few-shot of ground truth timing residuals. Particle swarm optimization algorithm is also used for automatic hyperparameter optimization, leading to improved prediction accuracy. Our solution, evaluated on the second data release of the International Pulsar Timing Array (IPTA), demonstrates robust generalization with accurate predictions in three metrics across high-frequency test frequency domains, while requiring only 10% of the timing residuals from these domains for model fine-tuning. Furthermore, our lightweight structure only costs 16.86 MB CPU memory and 18 milliseconds for single-step residual prediction. All these characteristics make our solution highly suitable for real-world applications, where effective and real-time predictions of pulsar timing residuals are essential-particularly in resource-constrained environments with limited computational power, memory, or energy availability.

</details>


### [254] [Set-Preserving Calibration from Conformal P-Values to E-Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03600)
*Nabil Alami,Jad Zakharia,Souhaib Ben Taieb*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的P2E校准器，将共形预测中的p值转换为e值而不改变预测集，解决了现有p-to-e校准器在CP设置中的保守性问题。该方法在交叉共形预测和共形聚合中实现了1-α覆盖保证并提升效率，为分布自由的不确定性量化开辟了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准共形预测依赖p值，但p值在处理依赖证据（如跨模型或数据分割的组合）时灵活性不足。现有e值公式与p值公式之间缺乏直接联系，且经典p-to-e校准器在CP设置中会导致预测集过于保守。

Method: 提出P2E校准器，将共形p值转换为e值，同时保持原始共形p值所诱导的预测集不变。理论上证明该校准器是集合保持的，并在经验上验证其效率优于现有p-to-e校准器。将该e值公式应用于交叉共形预测和共形聚合，利用e值合并和随机化的最新进展。

Result: P2E校准器在保持预测集不变的前提下显著提高了效率。在交叉共形预测和共形聚合应用中，基于e值的方法满足了1-α覆盖保证，同时比标准基线方法更高效。

Conclusion: 本文建立了共形预测中p值和e值公式之间的直接联系，提出的P2E校准器扩展了CP的灵活性，为高效、分布自由的 uncertainty quantification 提供了新方向。

Abstract: Standard conformal prediction (CP) procedures are typically formulated in terms of p-values, but reliance on p-values alone limits flexibility, for example, when combining dependent evidence across models or data splits. Recent work has explored e-value formulations for conformal inference, yet a direct connection between p- and e-value formulations in CP has been missing, especially regarding their statistical efficiency. We first identify limitations of classical p-to-e calibrators in the CP setting, showing that they are not set-preserving and can lead to overly conservative prediction sets. To address this, we propose a novel P2E calibrator that converts conformal p-values into e-values without altering the prediction set induced by the original conformal p-value. We establish both theoretically and empirically that our calibrator can yield significant efficiency gains over existing p-to-e calibrators. This e-value formulation enables principled use of recent advances in e-value merging and randomization, where we demonstrate its impact in two applications: cross-conformal prediction (CCP), whose variants typically provide only approximate $1-2α$ coverage, and conformal aggregation (CA). In both cases, our e-value-based methods satisfy the desired $1-α$ coverage guarantee while improving efficiency over standard baselines. More broadly, our approach expands the flexibility of CP and opens new directions for efficient, distribution-free uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [255] [Resource-Constrained Adaptive Inference for Sequential Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03736)
*Ruicheng Ao,Jiashuo Jiang,David Simchi-Levi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了资源受限定价控制器导致固定价格推断不可行的问题，提出了一种目标感知定价控制器，通过支持排除失败的形式化、局部去偏和诊断性弃权机制，实现了基于信息时钟的遗憾-信息核算，并证明多项式目标质量可带来多项式速率，而纯1/t目标分支若无额外局部移动则无法收缩固定目标区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限的定价控制器可能使固定价格推断变得不可能：控制器的资源状态可能将目标价格邻域从可行集中移除，即使每个已实现动作已知具有正密度。本文旨在形式化这种支持排除失败，并设计能够保证可行目标带并记录连续局部密度的控制器。

Method: 通过局部非识别结果和实现信息时钟形式化支持排除失败；设计目标感知定价控制器来认证可行目标带并记录连续局部密度；使用局部去偏方法生成学生化区间，其宽度由信息时钟控制；进行遗憾-信息核算，考虑预求解再优化误差。

Result: 多项式目标质量给出多项式速率，而纯1/t目标分支若无额外局部移动则无法产生收缩的固定目标区间。实验在认证频带中显示校准，并在资源状态崩溃目标支持时进行诊断性弃权。

Conclusion: 廉价探索可能不足以进行推断：需要额外的局部移动才能实现固定目标区间的收缩。所提出的框架通过诊断性弃权机制在资源约束下提供了可靠的推断能力。

Abstract: Resource-constrained pricing controllers can make fixed-price inference impossible: the controller's resource state may remove the target price neighborhood from the feasible set, even when every realized action has a known positive density. We formalize this support-exclusion failure through a local non-identification result and a realized information clock. We then design a target-aware pricing controller that certifies feasible target bands and logs continuous local densities. Localized debiasing gives studentized intervals whose width is governed by this clock. The resulting regret--information accounting, stated up to pilot re-solving error, shows that cheap exploration can be insufficient for inference: polynomial target mass gives polynomial rates, while a pure $1/t$ target branch does not yield shrinking fixed-target intervals without additional local movement. Experiments show calibration in certified bands and diagnostic abstention when the resource state collapses target support.

</details>


### [256] [A Quantitative Approximation Framework for Flow Distillation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03820)
*Weiguo Gao,Ming Li,Lei Shi,Hanfei Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了扩散蒸馏的定量近似框架，将少步采样视为学习流映射组合下的误差传播。通过理论分析和实验验证，揭示了低噪声多模态区域中局部近似误差会被动力刚性问题强烈放大，并提出了基于稳定性平衡的非均匀时间网格方法，在8段划分下相对MSE降低高达51.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的少步采样（蒸馏）在应用中至关重要，但现有方法缺乏对误差传播和放大机制的定量理解。本文旨在建立严格的数学框架，解释为什么某些情况下蒸馏效果不佳（如低噪声多模态区域），并为设计更高效的采样策略提供理论指导。

Method: 1) 将少步采样建模为学习流映射组合下的误差传播；2) 在高斯混合Ornstein-Uhlenbeck可解析设定中，分离近似误差（ReLU-ReQU网络对分数场的L^p(p_t)逼近保证）和稳定性误差（概率流ODE的Lipschitz常数L(t)及流映射稳定性估计）；3) 提出稳定性平衡的非均匀时间网格，通过累积稳定性坐标均匀划分实现。

Result: 理论方面：证明了ReLU-ReQU网络能均匀逼近高斯混合分数场，深度和宽度关于精度呈多对数增长；推导了概率流速度的Lipschitz界及流映射稳定性放大因子；识别了Lipschitz失配机制下一步蒸馏的结构性劣势。实验方面：非均匀时间网格在8段划分下相对MSE降低51.9%。

Conclusion: 扩散蒸馏中的误差放大主要源于低噪声多模态区域的动力学刚性；稳定性平衡的非均匀时间网格能显著提升蒸馏性能；理论框架为理解扩散蒸馏的瓶颈和设计更优采样策略提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: We develop a quantitative approximation framework for diffusion distillation, viewing few-step sampling as error propagation under compositions of learned flow maps. Focusing on trajectory distillation for the probability-flow ODE, we show that local approximation errors can be strongly amplified in low-noise multimodal regimes, where the underlying dynamics become stiff. In an analytically tractable Gaussian-mixture Ornstein--Uhlenbeck setting, we separate two core difficulties: approximating the time-dependent score field and controlling the dynamical amplification governed by the time-integrated Jacobian bound of the probability-flow ODE. On the approximation side, we prove constructive L^p(p_t) guarantees showing that ReLU--ReQU networks approximate the Gaussian-mixture score uniformly over time, with depth and width scaling polylogarithmically in the target accuracy and explicitly with the mixture geometry. On the stability side, we derive an explicit bound L(t) for the spatial Lipschitz constant of the probability-flow velocity and convert it into a flow map stability estimate governed by \int_s^t L(u)\,du, making late-time amplification in stiff regimes computable. Building on these estimates, we prove that deep residual compositions efficiently approximate the long-horizon transport, with global error controlled by the stability amplification factor, and identify a Lipschitz-mismatch regime in which one-step distillation is structurally unfavorable. The resulting theory yields a stability-balanced non-uniform time grid obtained by uniform partitioning in the cumulative stability coordinate. Experiments support the prediction and reduce end-to-end relative MSE by up to 51.9\% with 8 segments compared with uniform grids.

</details>


### [257] [Privacy-Robust Incrementality Measurement for Advertising Systems under Signal Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03878)
*Prashant Shekhar,Caroline Howard*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个鲁棒因果决策框架，用于在隐私保护导致的多种信号损失（匹配率、可链接性、归因窗口、聚合阈值、随机噪声、异质性）下评估广告增量性，给出清晰决策边界：外部报告可统一认证或拒绝，内部报告信息不足。基于Criteo和Hillstrom数据集验证，轻度退化可存活认证，但加入联合不确定性后所有压力测试均无法解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广告平台依赖随机提升测试测量增量性，但隐私保护报告系统会通过多种机制（匹配率损失、可链接性损失、归因窗口损失、聚合阈值抑制、随机报告噪声、分段异质性信号损失）导致观测信号退化，使得增量性判断不再可靠。现有方法缺乏一个系统性框架来应对这些隐私诱导的信号损失对因果推断的影响。

Method: 将隐私约束下的广告测量形式化为一个鲁棒因果决策问题：给定随机实验和隐私诱导退化的模糊集，框架将观测兼容的干净/未过滤实验世界的纤维投影到增量性泛函上，返回认证、拒绝和未解决三种决策。主要结果给出一个尖锐的决策边界，外部报告支持统一有效的认证或拒绝，内部报告信息不足以区分超过阈值的增量性与非增量性。

Result: 在200万条Criteo提升数据集和6.4万条Hillstrom邮件实验数据上，干净转化提升均为正（0.00112和0.00495）。种群级认证在Criteo的轻度退化和Hillstrom的严重退化下仍可存活，但所有有限样本压力设置在同时考虑不确定性和报告噪声后均无法解决。

Conclusion: 该研究为隐私感知的增量性测量贡献了一个决策理论层，其输出是由退化广告信号所能证明的最强因果主张。提供了有限样本认证、样本复杂度保证、极小化下界（显示信号损失降低有效信息）以及报告粒度权衡等辅助结果。

Abstract: Advertising platforms use randomized lift tests to measure incrementality, but privacy-preserving reporting systems degrade the observed signal through match-rate loss, linkability loss, attribution-window loss, aggregation-threshold suppression, randomized reporting noise, and segment-heterogeneous signal loss. This paper formulates privacy-constrained advertising measurement as a robust causal decision problem under the mentioned signal losses. Given a randomized experiment and an ambiguity set for privacy-induced degradation, the framework projects the observation-compatible fiber of clean/unfiltered experimental worlds onto the incrementality functional and returns certified, rejected, and unresolved decisions. The main result gives a sharp decision frontier. Reports outside the frontier support uniformly valid certification or rejection, whereas reports inside it contain too little information for any method to uniformly distinguish above-threshold incrementality from non-incrementality. Supporting results give finite-sample certification, sample-complexity guarantees, a minimax lower bound showing that signal loss reduces effective information, and a reporting-granularity tradeoff. On 2.0M Criteo Uplift rows and the 64K-row Hillstrom email experiment, clean conversion lift is positive in both datasets, with lifts 0.00112 and 0.00495, respectively. Population certification survives mild degradation in Criteo and severe degradation in Hillstrom, while all considered finite-sample stress settings in both datasets remain unresolved after simultaneous uncertainty and reporting noise are included. Overall, the research contributes a decision-theoretic layer for privacy-aware incrementality measurement whose output is the strongest causal-claim justified by degraded ads signals.

</details>
